================================================================================
MOIFONE DATABASE — TABLE INDEXES & METADATA
================================================================================
Use this file as the single reference for table purposes, keys, indexes, and
scope (company/branch). When you add or change a table, provide the same
fields and they will be merged here.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.account_head_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores company-wise chart of accounts
Primary key: account_id

Foreign keys:
  - parent_acc_id -> accounts.account_head_master(account_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  No

Indexes:
  - idx_account_head_master_company_id     (company_id)
  - idx_account_head_master_parent_acc_id  (parent_acc_id)
  - idx_account_head_master_account_no     (account_no)

Notes:
  - Company-level table (no branch column).
  - Self-referential parent-child hierarchy via parent_acc_id.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.accounts_parameter
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores company/branch-wise accounts configuration
Primary key: id

Foreign keys:
  - account_id -> accounts.account_head_master(account_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_parameter_company_id     (company_id)
  - idx_accounts_parameter_branch_id      (branch_id)
  - idx_accounts_parameter_account_id     (account_id)
  - idx_accounts_parameter_parameter_name (parameter_name)

Notes:
  - Branch-wise settings table (both company and branch apply).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.accounts_tally_parameter
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Company-level accounting parameter settings for Tally integration.
              Maps or configures values needed when syncing ERP accounting
              with Tally (e.g. parameter names, linked ledger names, numeric
              references, integration descriptions).

Primary key: id

Columns:
  - id              BIGSERIAL      NOT NULL  Primary key
  - company_id      BIGINT         NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - parameter_name  VARCHAR(250)   NULL      Tally / integration parameter name
  - ledger_name     VARCHAR(250)   NULL      Related ledger name
  - numeric_value   NUMERIC(18,0)  NULL      Numeric value when applicable
  - description     VARCHAR(500)   NULL      Notes or explanation
  - created_at      TIMESTAMP      NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at      TIMESTAMP      NOT NULL  Row last updated

Foreign keys:
  - company_id -> master company table (define when company master is finalized)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; parameters isolated per company)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level config; add branch later if business rules need it)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_tally_parameter_company_id       (company_id)
  - idx_accounts_tally_parameter_parameter_name   (parameter_name)
  - idx_accounts_tally_parameter_company_parameter (company_id, parameter_name)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: AccountsParameterTable; PostgreSQL name accounts_tally_parameter
    to reflect Tally-specific integration (not a blind MSSQL copy).
  - Naming: snake_case, schema-based modular structure.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.bank_account_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Company bank account names for accounting and payments (vouchers,
              receipt/payment, bank configuration, finance master).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - account_name

Columns:
  - id            BIGSERIAL     NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id    BIGINT        NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - account_name  VARCHAR(100)  NOT NULL  Bank account name
  - created_at    TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at    TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row last updated

Foreign keys:
  - company_id -> company master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_bank_account_master_company_account_name (company_id, account_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level master; branch assignment later via extension/mapping if needed)

Indexes:
  - idx_bank_account_master_company_id   (company_id)
  - idx_bank_account_master_account_name (account_name)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: BankAccountTable; PostgreSQL: accounts.bank_account_master.
  - Redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK, company_id, audit timestamps (created_at, updated_at).
  - AccountName -> account_name; removed MSSQL default '0' on account name (not meaningful).
  - Company-level accounting master; branch_id intentionally omitted from base design.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.cash_transaction_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Header for cash transactions: party, amount, type, cheque data,
              payment mode, voucher link, status (customer/supplier receipt or
              payment, cheque flows, bill settlement header, voucher posting).

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - transaction_id — unique per company
  - transaction_no — unique per (company_id, branch_id)

Columns:
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch identifier (legacy StationID)
  - transaction_id         BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business transaction id
  - transaction_no         NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Transaction number
  - transaction_date       TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Transaction date/time
  - counter_no             INTEGER         NULL      Counter where transaction occurred
  - customer_id            BIGINT          NULL      Linked customer
  - supplier_id            BIGINT          NULL      Linked supplier
  - amount                 NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Total transaction amount
  - transaction_type       VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Transaction type
  - post_dated_cheque      BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Post-dated cheque flag
  - cheque_details         VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Cheque reference / details
  - cheque_date            TIMESTAMP       NULL      Cheque date
  - total_current_amount   NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Total current amount (allocation)
  - total_paid_amount      NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Total paid amount
  - remarks                VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Remarks
  - status                 VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Transaction status
  - created_by             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by user
  - created_on             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Last modified by
  - modified_on            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modified at
  - counter_close_status   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Counter close state
  - salesman_id            BIGINT          NULL      Linked salesman
  - server_status          VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Legacy server/sync status
  - payment_mode           VARCHAR(15)     NOT NULL  e.g. CASH
  - voucher_master_id      BIGINT          NULL      Linked voucher master

Foreign keys:
  - company_id        -> company master (when finalized)
  - branch_id         -> branch master (when finalized)
  - customer_id       -> customer master (when finalized)
  - supplier_id       -> supplier master (when finalized)
  - salesman_id       -> salesman/agent/staff (when finalized)
  - voucher_master_id -> accounts.voucher_master(company_id, branch_id, voucher_master_id) (when enforced)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_cash_transaction_master_company_transaction_id (company_id, transaction_id)
  - uq_cash_transaction_master_company_transaction_no (company_id, branch_id, transaction_no)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; transactional finance, branch reporting/reconciliation)

Indexes:
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_company_id            (company_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_branch_id             (branch_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_transaction_date      (transaction_date)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_customer_id           (customer_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_supplier_id           (supplier_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_transaction_type      (transaction_type)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_status                (status)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_payment_mode          (payment_mode)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_voucher_master_id     (voucher_master_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_company_branch_date   (company_id, branch_id, transaction_date)
  - idx_cash_transaction_master_company_branch_status (company_id, branch_id, status)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: CashTransactionMaster; PostgreSQL: accounts.cash_transaction_master.
  - MSSQL PK was TransactionID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + transaction_id; adds company_id, branch_id.
  - StationID -> branch_id; CounterNo kept as counter_no (operational).
  - money -> NUMERIC(18,2); bit -> BOOLEAN for post_dated_cheque.
  - Column renames per redesign (TransactionNo, TransactionDate, CustomerID, SupplierID,
    TransactionType, PostDatedCheque, ChequeDetails, ChequeDate, TotalCurrentAmount,
    TotalPaidAmount, audit fields, CounterCloseStatus, SalesManID, ServerStatus,
    PaymentMode, VoucherMasterID -> snake_case).
  - Removed bad MSSQL defaults ('0', '1/1/1900', etc.).
  - Modular redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.cash_transaction_child
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Bill-wise detail lines for a cash transaction: allocation to one or
              more invoices/bills (invoice vs paid vs balance, settlement,
              ledger-linked detail).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - transaction_child_id

Parent reference:
  - transaction_id -> parent cash transaction / master entry

Columns:
  - id                    BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch identifier
  - transaction_child_id  BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business child transaction id
  - transaction_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent transaction id
  - bill_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Linked bill/invoice id
  - bill_date             TIMESTAMP       NULL      Bill or invoice date
  - invoice_no            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Invoice number
  - invoice_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Total invoice amount
  - current_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Current / outstanding amount
  - paid_amount           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Paid in this transaction
  - balance               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Remaining balance
  - ledger_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Related ledger id
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by user
  - created_on            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Last modified by
  - modified_on           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modified at

Foreign keys:
  - company_id     -> company master (when finalized)
  - branch_id      -> branch master (when finalized)
  - transaction_id -> parent cash transaction master (when finalized)
  - ledger_id      -> account/ledger master (when finalized)
  - bill_id        -> bill/invoice table (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_cash_transaction_child_company_child_id (company_id, transaction_child_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; transactional accounting, branch reporting/reconciliation)

Indexes:
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_company_id              (company_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_branch_id               (branch_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_transaction_id          (transaction_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_bill_id                 (bill_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_ledger_id               (ledger_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_bill_date               (bill_date)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_company_branch_transaction (company_id, branch_id, transaction_id)
  - idx_cash_transaction_child_company_ledger          (company_id, ledger_id)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: CashTransactionChild; PostgreSQL: accounts.cash_transaction_child.
  - MSSQL had identity ID + TransactionChildID; redesign keeps id PK + transaction_child_id.
  - Adds company_id, branch_id; money -> NUMERIC(18,2).
  - Column renames: BillID, BillDate, InvoiceNo, InvoiceAmount, CurrentAmount, PaidAmount,
    LedgerID, CrBy/CrOn/ModBy/ModOn -> snake_case as listed in source spec.
  - Removed legacy defaults such as '0' and '1/1/1900' (not good PostgreSQL defaults).
  - Modular redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.credit_card_type_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Supported credit/payment card types for payments and accounting
              (sales payment mode, settlement, transaction reference, company
              payment configuration).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - credit_card_id
  - credit_card_name

Columns:
  - id                 BIGSERIAL     NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id         BIGINT        NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - credit_card_id     BIGINT        NOT NULL  Business card type id
  - credit_card_name   VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  Card type name
  - status             VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  e.g. ACTIVE / INACTIVE
  - created_at         TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at         TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row last updated

Foreign keys:
  - company_id -> company master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_credit_card_type_master_company_card_id   (company_id, credit_card_id)
  - uq_credit_card_type_master_company_card_name (company_id, credit_card_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level payment master, not branch transactional data)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_credit_card_type_master_company_id (company_id)
  - idx_accounts_credit_card_type_master_card_name  (credit_card_name)
  - idx_accounts_credit_card_type_master_status      (status)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: CreditCardTypeTable; PostgreSQL: accounts.credit_card_type_master.
  - MSSQL PK was CreditCardID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + credit_card_id; adds company_id,
    status, timestamps; CreditCardID/Name -> snake_case.
  - Removed legacy: UploadStatus, ServerStatus, StationID (not in clean company-level design).
  - Modular redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.transaction_expense_detail
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Expense line under an accounting transaction: ledger, dates, reference,
              foreign and base amounts, expense/transaction type, tax rate, server
              status — accounts-schema financial detail.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.TransactionExpenseTable (PK TransactionExpenseID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business key (unique per company and branch):
  - transaction_expense_id (legacy TransactionExpenseID)

Columns:
  - id                        BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id                BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - branch_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch (new)
  - transaction_expense_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business line id (legacy TransactionExpenseID)
  - transaction_master_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent header (legacy TransactionMasterID)
  - ledger_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Ledger/account (legacy LedgerID)
  - description               VARCHAR(250)    NULL      Line text (legacy Description)
  - reference_no              VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Voucher/ref (legacy RefNo)
  - transaction_date          TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Tx date (legacy TransDate)
  - foreign_currency_id       BIGINT          NULL      FC ref (legacy FCurrencyID)
  - foreign_currency_rate     NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  FX rate (legacy FCurrencyRate)
  - foreign_amount            NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  FC amount (legacy FAmount)
  - amount                    NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Base/local amount (legacy Amount)
  - transaction_type          VARCHAR(25)     NULL      Tx category (legacy TransType)
  - expense_type              VARCHAR(25)     NULL      Expense class (legacy ExpenseType)
  - server_status             VARCHAR(25)     NULL      Server state (legacy ServerStatus)
  - tax_rate                  NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax % (legacy TaxRate)
  - created_at                TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (new)
  - created_by                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (new)
  - modified_at               TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (new)
  - modified_by               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (new)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - (company_id, branch_id, transaction_master_id) -> accounts.transaction_master(company_id, branch_id, transaction_master_id)
    when that header table and keys are finalized
  - ledger_id -> accounts.account_head_master (or chart key) when linkage is finalized

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_accounts_transaction_expense_detail_company_branch_expense_id  (company_id, branch_id, transaction_expense_id)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - foreign_currency_rate >= 0; foreign_amount >= 0; amount >= 0; tax_rate >= 0

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_transaction_expense_detail_company_branch               (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_accounts_transaction_expense_detail_company_branch_master        (company_id, branch_id, transaction_master_id)
  - idx_accounts_transaction_expense_detail_company_branch_ledger        (company_id, branch_id, ledger_id)
  - idx_accounts_transaction_expense_detail_company_branch_date          (company_id, branch_id, transaction_date)
  - idx_accounts_transaction_expense_detail_company_branch_trans_type    (company_id, branch_id, transaction_type)

Notes:
  - TransactionExpenseTable -> accounts.transaction_expense_detail; money -> NUMERIC(18,2); RefNo -> reference_no;
    TransDate -> transaction_date; FCurrency* -> foreign_currency_*; standard audit added; parent/ledger FKs when masters exist; snake_case.

# accounts.vat_nature_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     VAT nature-of-transaction / classification for reporting and compliance
              mapping — accounts-schema setup (distinct from percentage in core.vat_group_master).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.VATNatureTable (PK NatureOfTrnsID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - vat_nature_id (legacy NatureOfTrnsID)
  - vat_nature_name (legacy NatureOfTransactionName)

Columns:
  - id                BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - vat_nature_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business id (legacy NatureOfTrnsID)
  - vat_nature_name   VARCHAR(100)    NOT NULL  Name (legacy NatureOfTransactionName)
  - vat_nature_type   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Category/type (legacy Type)
  - record_status     VARCHAR(10)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (new)
  - created_at        TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (new)
  - created_by        VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (new)
  - modified_at       TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (new)
  - modified_by       VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (new)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_accounts_vat_nature_master_company_vat_nature_id   (company_id, vat_nature_id)
  - uq_accounts_vat_nature_master_company_vat_nature_name  (company_id, vat_nature_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level compliance / reporting setup)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_vat_nature_master_company_id               (company_id)
  - idx_accounts_vat_nature_master_company_type             (company_id, vat_nature_type)
  - idx_accounts_vat_nature_master_company_record_status  (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - VATNatureTable -> accounts.vat_nature_master; NatureOfTrnsID -> vat_nature_id; Type -> vat_nature_type;
    not the same as core.vat_group_master (rates); no legacy FK to VAT group; record_status + audit added; snake_case.

# accounts.voucher_type_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Voucher type setup: code, name, alias, prefix, numbering method, and
              number-reset date — company-level accounting configuration (supports
              accounts.voucher_master via voucher_type_id).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.VoucherTypeMaster (PK VoucherTypeID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - voucher_type_id (legacy VoucherTypeID)
  - voucher_type_code (legacy VoucherTypeIDString)
  - voucher_name (legacy VoucherName)

Columns:
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - voucher_type_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business type id (legacy VoucherTypeID)
  - voucher_type_code      VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Type code/string (legacy VoucherTypeIDString)
  - voucher_name           VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Type name (legacy VoucherName)
  - voucher_name_alias     VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Alias (legacy VoucherNameAlias)
  - voucher_prefix         VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Prefix (legacy VoucherPrefix)
  - numbering_method       VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Numbering mode (legacy NoMethod)
  - voucher_no_reset_date  TIMESTAMP       NULL      Reset/ref date (legacy VoucherNoRestDate)
  - record_status          VARCHAR(15)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (new)
  - created_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (new)
  - created_by             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (new)
  - modified_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (new)
  - modified_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (new)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_accounts_voucher_type_master_company_type_id     (company_id, voucher_type_id)
  - uq_accounts_voucher_type_master_company_type_code   (company_id, voucher_type_code)
  - uq_accounts_voucher_type_master_company_type_name   (company_id, voucher_name)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - numbering_method IN ('AUTO','MANUAL','BOTH','SYSTEM')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level setup; voucher transactions stay branch-scoped on voucher_master)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_voucher_type_master_company_id             (company_id)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_type_master_company_record_status  (company_id, record_status)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_type_master_company_prefix         (company_id, voucher_prefix)

Notes:
  - VoucherTypeMaster -> accounts.voucher_type_master; VoucherTypeIDString -> voucher_type_code; NoMethod -> numbering_method;
    VoucherNoRestDate -> voucher_no_reset_date; record_status + audit added; create before enforcing voucher_master FK on voucher_type_id;
    ties to accounts.voucher_master through (company_id, voucher_type_id); snake_case.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts.voucher_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Accounting voucher header: type, auto/manual numbers and prefix, date,
              reference, amount, posting state, creation mode, posted linkage,
              counter-close reference — company + branch scoped transactions.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.VoucherMaster (PK VoucherMasterID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company and branch):
  - voucher_master_id (legacy VoucherMasterID)
  - (company_id, branch_id, voucher_type_id, auto_voucher_no)
  - (company_id, branch_id, voucher_type_id, manual_voucher_no)

Columns:
  - id                   BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - branch_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch (legacy StationID)
  - voucher_master_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business voucher header id (legacy VoucherMasterID)
  - voucher_type_id      BIGINT          NOT NULL  Voucher type (legacy VoucherTypeID)
  - auto_voucher_no      BIGINT          NOT NULL  System voucher no. (legacy AutoVoucherNo)
  - manual_voucher_no    VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Manual voucher no. (legacy ManualVoucherNo)
  - voucher_prefix       VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Prefix (legacy VoucherPrefix)
  - voucher_date         TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Voucher date/time (legacy VoucherDate)
  - reference_no         VARCHAR(50)     NULL      External ref (legacy RefNo)
  - voucher_amount       NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Total amount (legacy VoucherAmount)
  - remarks              VARCHAR(250)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remark)
  - post_status          VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Posting state (legacy PostStatus)
  - creation_mode        VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Source/mode (legacy CreationMode)
  - voucher_posted_id    BIGINT          NULL      Posted linkage (legacy VoucherPostedID)
  - counter_close_no     VARCHAR(15)     NULL      Counter close ref (legacy CounterCloseNo)
  - record_status        VARCHAR(15)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (legacy RStatusM)
  - created_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at          TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by          VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys:
  - (company_id, voucher_type_id) -> accounts.voucher_type_master(company_id, voucher_type_id) (recommended when enforced)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id -> core.branch_master (when finalized)
  - voucher_posted_id -> accounts.voucher_master(company_id, branch_id, voucher_master_id) (self / posted ref when enforced)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_voucher_master_id    (company_id, branch_id, voucher_master_id)
  - uq_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_type_auto_no         (company_id, branch_id, voucher_type_id, auto_voucher_no)
  - uq_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_type_manual_no       (company_id, branch_id, voucher_type_id, manual_voucher_no)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - voucher_amount >= 0
  - post_status IN ('DRAFT','POSTED','CANCELLED','APPROVED','PENDING')
  - record_status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','DELETED')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch                 (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_date            (company_id, branch_id, voucher_date)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_type            (company_id, branch_id, voucher_type_id)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_post_status     (company_id, branch_id, post_status)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_master_company_branch_counter_close   (company_id, branch_id, counter_close_no)

Notes:
  - VoucherMaster -> accounts.voucher_master; StationID -> branch_id; RefNo -> reference_no; Remark -> remarks;
    RStatusM -> record_status; money -> NUMERIC(18,2); company_id added; numbering unique per type + branch;
    counter_close_no: preserve legacy default/business PENDING where applicable; if manual_voucher_no is often NULL,
    enforce manual uniqueness with a partial unique index WHERE manual_voucher_no IS NOT NULL; snake_case.

# accounts.voucher_detail
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Voucher line/detail: ledger account, debit/credit amounts, outstanding
              balance, narration, posting and record status — tied to branch-scoped
              voucher header.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.VoucherChild (PK VoucherChildID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company and branch):
  - voucher_detail_id (legacy VoucherChildID)

Columns:
  - id                    BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - branch_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch (new; aligns with voucher_master)
  - voucher_detail_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business line id (legacy VoucherChildID)
  - voucher_master_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent header (legacy VoucherMasterID)
  - account_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Ledger/account (legacy AccountID)
  - credit_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Credit (legacy CrAmount)
  - debit_amount          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Debit (legacy DrAmount)
  - outstanding_balance   NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  OS balance (legacy OsBalance)
  - narration             VARCHAR(250)    NULL      Line text (legacy Naration)
  - post_status           VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Posting state (legacy PostStatus)
  - record_status         VARCHAR(15)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (legacy RStatusC)
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, voucher_master_id) -> accounts.voucher_master(company_id, branch_id, voucher_master_id)
    (recommended when enforced)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - account_id -> accounts.account_head_master (company_id, account_id) when chart keys are finalized

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_accounts_voucher_detail_company_branch_voucher_detail_id   (company_id, branch_id, voucher_detail_id)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - credit_amount >= 0; debit_amount >= 0; outstanding_balance >= 0
  - chk_accounts_voucher_detail_credit_debit_rule:
      (credit_amount > 0 AND debit_amount = 0)
      OR (debit_amount > 0 AND credit_amount = 0)
      OR (credit_amount = 0 AND debit_amount = 0)
  - post_status IN ('DRAFT','POSTED','CANCELLED','APPROVED','PENDING')
  - record_status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','DELETED')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; matches voucher header scope)

Indexes:
  - idx_accounts_voucher_detail_company_branch              (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_detail_company_branch_master       (company_id, branch_id, voucher_master_id)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_detail_company_branch_account      (company_id, branch_id, account_id)
  - idx_accounts_voucher_detail_company_branch_post_status  (company_id, branch_id, post_status)

Notes:
  - VoucherChild -> accounts.voucher_detail; CrAmount/DrAmount -> credit_amount/debit_amount; OsBalance -> outstanding_balance;
    Naration -> narration; RStatusC -> record_status; money -> NUMERIC(18,2); company_id + branch_id added for isolation with header;
    debit/credit exclusivity check keeps lines one-sided or both zero; account_head FK when master keys are stable; snake_case.

# biz.customer_contact
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores multiple contact persons for a customer, including billing,
              delivery, and primary communication contacts. Enables tracking of different
              contact roles within a single customer organization.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business key (unique per company):
  - customer_contact_id

Columns:
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - customer_contact_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business contact identifier
  - customer_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent customer reference
  - contact_name           VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Contact person full name
  - designation            VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Role or job title
  - department             VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Department or division
  - mobile_no              VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Mobile phone number
  - whatsapp_no            VARCHAR(30)     NULL      WhatsApp number
  - phone_no               VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Landline or office phone
  - email                  VARCHAR(150)    NULL      Email address
  - is_primary_contact     BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Whether this is primary contact
  - is_billing_contact     BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Whether used for billing communications
  - is_delivery_contact    BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Whether used for delivery communications
  - is_active              BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active/inactive status
  - remarks                VARCHAR(300)    NULL      Optional notes or comments
  - created_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by             BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by            BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - customer_id   -> biz.customer_master (when finalized)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_customer_contact_company_contact_id   (company_id, customer_contact_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  No  (contacts belong to company-level customer record, not branch-scoped)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_customer_contact_company_id                    (company_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_contact_customer_id                   (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_contact_company_customer_primary      (company_id, customer_id, is_primary_contact)
  - idx_biz_customer_contact_email                         (company_id, email)
  - idx_biz_customer_contact_mobile_no                     (company_id, mobile_no)
  - idx_biz_customer_contact_is_active                     (company_id, is_active)

Notes:
  - One customer can have multiple contacts with different roles/responsibilities.
  - Boolean flags (is_primary_contact, is_billing_contact, is_delivery_contact) enable multi-role assignment.
  - Application can enforce business rule: only one primary contact per customer if needed.
  - Multiple billing or delivery contacts support distributed B2B organizations.
  - Email and mobile_no indexed for quick contact lookup and communication routing.
  - is_active allows soft-delete without removing historical contact records.

# biz.customer_address
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores multiple addresses for a customer such as billing, shipping, site,
              and general office addresses. Supports address selection in sales orders,
              delivery routing, and service/garage operations.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business key (unique per company):
  - customer_address_id

Columns:
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - customer_address_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business address identifier
  - customer_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent customer reference
  - address_type           VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Address classification: BILLING, SHIPPING, SITE, GENERAL
  - address_line1          VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Primary address line
  - address_line2          VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Secondary address line
  - city                   VARCHAR(100)    NULL      City name
  - state_name             VARCHAR(100)    NULL      State or region
  - country                VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Country name
  - po_box                 VARCHAR(30)     NULL      PO box number
  - postal_code            VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Postal code or ZIP
  - contact_name           VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Contact person at address
  - phone_no               VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Phone number
  - mobile_no              VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Mobile number
  - email                  VARCHAR(150)    NULL      Email address
  - is_default             BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Default address for type
  - is_active              BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active/inactive status
  - remarks                VARCHAR(300)    NULL      Optional notes
  - created_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by             BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by            BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - customer_id   -> biz.customer_master (when finalized)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_customer_address_company_address_id   (company_id, customer_address_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  No  (addresses belong to company-level customer record, shared across branches)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_customer_address_company_id                     (company_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_address_customer_id                    (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_address_company_customer_type          (company_id, customer_id, address_type)
  - idx_biz_customer_address_company_customer_default       (company_id, customer_id, is_default)
  - idx_biz_customer_address_is_active                      (company_id, is_active)

Notes:
  - One customer can have multiple addresses: billing, shipping, site/warehouse, and general office.
  - address_type enables classification for order/delivery routing without separate flags.
  - is_default flag allows quick selection of primary address for each type in sales/service operations.
  - Useful for: sales orders (billing/shipping address), delivery routing, garage service location, POS branch delivery.
  - Multiple contacts at same address supported via customer_contact table link.
  - Addresses remain available after customer inactivation via is_active flag for historical reference.
  - Customer master table retains current address fields as denormalized defaults for performance.

# biz.customer_interaction_log
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores all CRM communication history including calls, visits, emails,
              WhatsApp messages, meetings, and general customer/lead interaction records.
              Enables complete audit trail and CRM timeline screens for customer/lead history.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business key (unique per company):
  - interaction_id

Columns:
  - id                    BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Operational branch for the interaction
  - interaction_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business interaction identifier
  - customer_id           BIGINT          NULL      Linked customer (nullable; for leads use lead_id)
  - lead_id               BIGINT          NULL      Linked lead (nullable; for customers use customer_id)
  - interaction_date      TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Date and time of interaction
  - interaction_type      VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Type: CALL, MEETING, VISIT, EMAIL, WHATSAPP, SMS, etc.
  - interaction_mode      VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Mode: PHONE, INPERSON, ONLINE, EMAIL, etc.
  - subject               VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Subject or title of interaction
  - interaction_summary   TEXT            NULL      Detailed summary or notes
  - outcome               VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Result or outcome of interaction
  - staff_id              BIGINT          NULL      Responsible staff member
  - next_action_at        TIMESTAMP       NULL      Next action or follow-up due date
  - record_status         VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Status: ACTIVE, COMPLETED, CANCELLED
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by            BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by           BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id     -> core.station_master or branch master (when finalized)
  - customer_id   -> biz.customer_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - lead_id       -> biz.lead_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - staff_id      -> core.staff_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_customer_interaction_log_company_interaction_id   (company_id, interaction_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; operational tracking and branch-wise CRM reporting)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_company_id              (company_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_branch_id               (branch_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_customer_id             (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_lead_id                 (company_id, lead_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_interaction_date        (company_id, interaction_date)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_staff_id                (company_id, staff_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_next_action_at          (company_id, next_action_at)
  - idx_biz_customer_interaction_log_record_status           (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - Either customer_id or lead_id should be populated; both can be null only in edge cases.
  - Immutable audit trail: created_by and created_at capture original entry; modified_* for updates only.
  - interaction_type and interaction_mode support classification for reporting (calls per week, meetings per month, etc.).
  - next_action_at enables task/reminder systems and CRM activity dashboards.
  - record_status tracks completion without deletion: ACTIVE (pending/ongoing), COMPLETED (closed), CANCELLED (discarded).
  - Useful for: CRM timeline screens, customer history, sales cycle analysis, staff performance metrics, follow-up management.
  - Branch assignment supports regional CRM team reporting and activity tracking across locations.
  - interaction_summary can store structured or free-text notes; support external attachments via separate table if needed later.

# biz.customer_followup
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores scheduled and completed follow-ups for customers, leads, and
              opportunities. Enables task management, reminder systems, and CRM
              activity dashboard widgets for sales team productivity.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business key (unique per company):
  - followup_id

Columns:
  - id                      BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Operational branch for follow-up ownership
  - followup_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business follow-up identifier
  - customer_id             BIGINT          NULL      Related customer (nullable)
  - lead_id                 BIGINT          NULL      Related lead (nullable)
  - opportunity_id          BIGINT          NULL      Related opportunity (nullable; future linkage)
  - followup_date           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Due date and time for follow-up
  - followup_type           VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Type: CALL, MEETING, VISIT, EMAIL, WHATSAPP, etc.
  - priority_level          VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Priority: LOW, NORMAL, HIGH, URGENT
  - subject                 VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Task subject or title
  - notes                   TEXT            NULL      Detailed task notes and instructions
  - assigned_to_staff_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Responsible staff member
  - completed_at            TIMESTAMP       NULL      Completion date/time (nullable; null if pending)
  - completion_notes        TEXT            NULL      Notes on how follow-up was completed
  - status                  VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Status: PENDING, COMPLETED, CANCELLED, OVERDUE
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by              BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by             BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id           -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id            -> core.station_master or branch master (when finalized)
  - customer_id          -> biz.customer_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - lead_id              -> biz.lead_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - opportunity_id       -> (future opportunity master table; nullable)
  - assigned_to_staff_id -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - created_by           -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by          -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_customer_followup_company_followup_id   (company_id, followup_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; follow-up ownership and branch-wise activity reporting)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_company_id                 (company_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_branch_id                  (branch_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_followup_date              (company_id, followup_date)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_status                     (company_id, status)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_assigned_to_staff_id       (company_id, assigned_to_staff_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_customer_id                (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_lead_id                    (company_id, lead_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_branch_followup_date       (branch_id, followup_date)
  - idx_biz_customer_followup_branch_status              (branch_id, status)

Notes:
  - opportunity_id intentionally included for future linkage when opportunity master table is finalized.
  - At least one of customer_id, lead_id, or opportunity_id should be populated; all three can be null only in edge cases.
  - status progression: PENDING (scheduled) -> COMPLETED (finished) or CANCELLED (dismissed); OVERDUE added by application logic based on current date.
  - priority_level (LOW/NORMAL/HIGH/URGENT) enables sorting and filtering in reminder widgets and dashboards.
  - assigned_to_staff_id enables task routing, team capacity analysis, and individual performance tracking.
  - followup_type supports categorization and scheduling optimization (group calls, meetings, visits).
  - completion_at and completion_notes capture execution history for sales cycle analysis and activity metrics.
  - Powers reminder widgets, CRM dashboards, mobile app notifications, and team activity boards.
  - Branch assignment enables regional team management, territory-based follow-up tracking, and location-specific reporting.
  - Can later be extended with status history and audit trail via separate change log table if needed.

# biz.customer_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Main customer / party entity for sales, accounts, CRM, garage,
              service: setup, billing/delivery, credit/limit/period, price level,
              tax reg, loyalty/type, contacts/address, balance/advance.

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - customer_id
  - customer_code

Columns:
  - id                            BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id                    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - customer_id                   BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business customer id
  - customer_code                 VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Customer code
  - customer_no_numeric           NUMERIC(18,0)   NULL      Legacy numeric ref
  - customer_name                 VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Customer name
  - customer_name_arabic          VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Name (Arabic)
  - address                       VARCHAR(300)    NULL      Address
  - address_arabic                VARCHAR(300)    NULL      Address (Arabic)
  - po_box                        VARCHAR(15)     NULL      PO Box
  - city_id                       BIGINT          NULL      Linked city
  - city_name                     VARCHAR(100)    NULL      City free-text fallback
  - city_name_arabic              VARCHAR(100)    NULL      City (Arabic)
  - country_id                    BIGINT          NULL      Linked country
  - country_name                  VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Country free-text fallback
  - telephone                     VARCHAR(15)     NULL      Telephone
  - fax                           VARCHAR(15)     NULL      Fax
  - email                         VARCHAR(75)     NULL      Email
  - contact_person                VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Main contact
  - designation                   VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Main contact designation
  - mobile_no                     VARCHAR(25)     NULL      Mobile
  - contact_person_2              VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Second contact
  - designation_2                 VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Second designation
  - mobile_no_2                   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Second mobile
  - payment_mode                  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Default payment mode
  - credit_balance                NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Credit balance
  - credit_limit                  NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Credit limit
  - credit_period                 NUMERIC(18,0)   NOT NULL  Credit period
  - price_level_id                BIGINT          NULL      Price level
  - remarks                       VARCHAR(750)    NULL      Remarks
  - credit_status                 VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Credit control status
  - loyalty_status                VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Loyalty status
  - customer_type                 VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Customer type/category
  - advance_amount                NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Advance amount
  - customer_tax_reg_no           VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Tax registration no.
  - customer_prefix               CHAR(1)         NULL      Customer prefix
  - labour_profit_percentage      NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Labour profit %
  - parts_profit_percentage       NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Parts profit %
  - sublet_profit_percentage      NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Sublet profit %
  - consumable_profit_percentage  NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Consumable profit %
  - lubricant_profit_percentage   NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Lubricant profit %
  - managed_by                    BIGINT          NULL      Account manager / staff
  - account_balance               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Account balance snapshot
  - mess_master_id                BIGINT          NULL      Mess master link
  - mess_start_date               TIMESTAMP       NULL      Mess start date
  - status                        VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. ACTIVE / INACTIVE
  - created_by                    VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by
  - created_on                    TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by                   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by
  - modified_on                   TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at

Foreign keys:
  - company_id      -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - city_id         -> core.city_master (when finalized)
  - country_id      -> core.country_master (when finalized)
  - price_level_id  -> price level master (when finalized)
  - managed_by      -> staff/user master (when finalized)
  - mess_master_id  -> mess master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_customer_master_company_customer_id   (company_id, customer_id)
  - uq_customer_master_company_customer_code   (company_id, customer_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level master; branch ownership via separate mapping if needed)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_customer_master_company_id        (company_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_customer_name     (customer_name)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_mobile_no         (mobile_no)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_email             (email)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_city_id           (city_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_country_id        (country_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_price_level_id    (price_level_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_managed_by        (managed_by)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_status            (status)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_credit_status     (credit_status)
  - idx_biz_customer_master_company_name      (company_id, customer_name)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: CustomerMaster; PostgreSQL: biz.customer_master.
  - MSSQL PK was CustomerID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + customer_id; CompanyID -> company_id.
  - StationID removed; UploadStatus, ServerStatus removed.
  - Address: city_id/country_id + optional free-text fallbacks (Arabic fields retained).
  - money -> NUMERIC(18,2); profit fields NUMERIC(5,2).
  - Examples: CustomerTaxRegNo, LoyalityStatus->loyalty_status, Arabic/mobile/mess columns -> snake_case.
  - Removed legacy defaults ('0', blanks, 1/1/1900). Modular redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.

# biz.customer_note
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores general CRM notes attached to customers, leads, or opportunities.
              Enables internal collaboration, customer relationship history, and contextual
              documentation for CRM timeline and reference.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business key (unique per company):
  - customer_note_id

Columns:
  - id                 BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id         BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch context for operational visibility
  - customer_note_id   BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business note identifier
  - customer_id        BIGINT          NULL      Related customer (nullable)
  - lead_id            BIGINT          NULL      Related lead (nullable)
  - opportunity_id     BIGINT          NULL      Related opportunity (nullable)
  - note_date          TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Date and time of note
  - note_title         VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Optional note heading or title
  - note_text          TEXT            NOT NULL  Note content
  - is_important       BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Flag for important/high-priority notes
  - created_at         TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by         BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at        TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by        BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id     -> core.station_master or branch master (when finalized)
  - customer_id   -> biz.customer_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - lead_id       -> biz.lead_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - opportunity_id -> (future opportunity master table; nullable)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_customer_note_company_note_id   (company_id, customer_note_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; operational branch context and note visibility)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_customer_note_company_id        (company_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_note_branch_id         (branch_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_note_customer_id       (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_note_lead_id           (company_id, lead_id)
  - idx_biz_customer_note_note_date         (company_id, note_date)
  - idx_biz_customer_note_is_important      (company_id, is_important)

Notes:
  - At most one of customer_id, lead_id, or opportunity_id should be populated; enables flexible CRM documentation.
  - is_important flag enables filtering for priority review and dashboard highlighting.
  - note_text supports free-form documentation for internal collaboration and customer relationship history.
  - Branch assignment enables team-based visibility: notes visible to assigned branch for follow-up coordination.
  - Immutable audit trail: created_by/created_at capture original author; modified_* tracks updates.
  - Can later be extended with attachments table for files, images, documents linked to notes.
  - Useful for: internal comments, relationship history, special requirements, deal context, implementation notes.
  - Chronological note_date enables CRM timeline screens and historical relationship context.

  - idx_biz_loyalty_master_company_id              (company_id)
  - idx_biz_loyalty_master_branch_id               (branch_id)
  - idx_biz_loyalty_master_customer_id             (customer_id)
  - idx_biz_loyalty_master_loyalty_plan_id          (loyalty_plan_id)
  - idx_biz_loyalty_master_loyalty_barcode          (loyalty_barcode)
  - idx_biz_loyalty_master_company_branch_customer (company_id, branch_id, customer_id)

    FK to loyalty_plan_master as in source spec.

# biz.lead_source_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores the master list of lead sources used in CRM, such as Website,
              Walk-In, Referral, Social Media, Campaign, and Phone Inquiry. Company-level
              configuration for lead classification and tracking across sales pipeline.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business keys (unique per company):
  - lead_source_id
  - source_code
  - source_name

Columns:
  - id                    BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - lead_source_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business identifier for lead source
  - source_code           VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Stable code like WEBSITE, WALKIN, REFERRAL
  - source_name           VARCHAR(150)    NOT NULL  Display name of source
  - source_description    VARCHAR(300)    NULL      Optional description
  - is_active             BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active/inactive status
  - display_order         INTEGER         NOT NULL  Ordering in UI display
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by            BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by           BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_lead_source_master_company_lead_source_id   (company_id, lead_source_id)
  - uq_biz_lead_source_master_company_source_code      (company_id, source_code)
  - uq_biz_lead_source_master_company_source_name      (company_id, source_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; company-level CRM configuration)
  branch_id:  No  (shared across all branches of the company)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_lead_source_master_company_id              (company_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_source_master_is_active               (company_id, is_active)
  - idx_biz_lead_source_master_display_order           (company_id, display_order)
  - idx_biz_lead_source_master_source_code             (company_id, source_code)

Notes:
  - Keep as reusable company-level CRM master configuration.
  - Can later be linked to campaign, sales pipeline, or opportunity tracking tables if needed.
  - display_order enables UI dropdown/list ordering without hard-coded sequences.
  - is_active allows soft-delete behavior without removing historical lead references.
  - source_code provides stable system identifiers for integrations and reports.

# biz.lead_status_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores CRM lead statuses such as New, Contacted, Qualified, Not Interested,
              Converted, Lost. Supports lead workflow management and pipeline reporting
              with flexible status type grouping (OPEN/CLOSED/WON/LOST).

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business keys (unique per company):
  - lead_status_id
  - status_code
  - status_name

Columns:
  - id                BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - lead_status_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business status identifier
  - status_code       VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Stable code like NEW, CONTACTED, QUALIFIED, CONVERTED
  - status_name       VARCHAR(150)    NOT NULL  Display label for lead status
  - status_type       VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Broader grouping: OPEN, CLOSED, WON, LOST
  - is_final_status   BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Whether this status closes/finalizes the lead
  - is_active         BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active/inactive status
  - display_order     INTEGER         NOT NULL  UI display ordering
  - created_at        TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by        BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at       TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by       BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_lead_status_master_company_lead_status_id   (company_id, lead_status_id)
  - uq_biz_lead_status_master_company_status_code      (company_id, status_code)
  - uq_biz_lead_status_master_company_status_name      (company_id, status_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; company-level lead status configuration)
  branch_id:  No  (shared across all branches of the company)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_lead_status_master_company_id              (company_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_status_master_is_active               (company_id, is_active)
  - idx_biz_lead_status_master_status_type             (company_id, status_type)
  - idx_biz_lead_status_master_is_final                (company_id, is_final_status)

Notes:
  - Separate UI sequence (display_order) from business finality (is_final_status) for workflow flexibility.
  - status_type grouping enables higher-level workflow reporting and pipeline analytics.
  - is_final_status determines workflow state transitions: leads with final_status=true cannot change status.
  - Example progression: NEW -> CONTACTED -> QUALIFIED -> CONVERTED (final) or LOST (final).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# biz.opportunity_stage_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores opportunity pipeline stages such as Prospecting, Proposal Sent,
              Negotiation, Won, Lost. Supports sales pipeline tracking, probability-weighted
              forecasting, and stage-based reporting with distinction between closed and won stages.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business keys (unique per company):
  - opportunity_stage_id
  - stage_code
  - stage_name

Columns:
  - id                      BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - opportunity_stage_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business stage identifier
  - stage_code              VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Stable code like PROSPECTING, PROPOSAL, NEGOTIATION
  - stage_name              VARCHAR(150)    NOT NULL  Display name of pipeline stage
  - probability_percent     NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Estimated close probability (0-100%)
  - is_closed_stage         BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Whether this stage closes/ends the opportunity
  - is_won_stage            BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Whether this stage represents a won opportunity
  - is_active               BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active/inactive status
  - display_order           INTEGER         NOT NULL  UI display ordering
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by              BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by             BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id    -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - created_by    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by   -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_opportunity_stage_master_company_stage_id   (company_id, opportunity_stage_id)
  - uq_biz_opportunity_stage_master_company_stage_code (company_id, stage_code)
  - uq_biz_opportunity_stage_master_company_stage_name (company_id, stage_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; company-level CRM pipeline configuration)
  branch_id:  No  (shared across all branches of the company)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_opportunity_stage_master_company_id        (company_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_stage_master_is_active         (company_id, is_active)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_stage_master_display_order     (company_id, display_order)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_stage_master_is_closed         (company_id, is_closed_stage)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_stage_master_is_won            (company_id, is_won_stage)

Notes:
  - probability_percent drives weighted pipeline forecasting: higher probability stages contribute more to revenue forecast.
  - is_closed_stage and is_won_stage allow explicit distinction: some opportunities close as LOST (closed but not won).
  - display_order controls UI pipeline visualization without hard-coded sequences.
  - Example pipeline: PROSPECTING (10%) -> PROPOSAL (30%) -> NEGOTIATION (60%) -> WON (100%, closed, won) or LOST (0%, closed, not won).
  - Supports later integration with weighted opportunity values and sales forecast reporting.
