# biz.lead_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Main CRM lead table used to store enquiries, prospects, and potential
              customers before conversion to confirmed customers or opportunities.
              Tracks lead lifecycle: creation, assignment, follow-up, and conversion/closure.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business keys (unique per company):
  - lead_id
  - lead_code

Columns:
  - id                      BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Operational branch for lead management
  - lead_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business lead identifier
  - lead_code               VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Human-readable lead reference code
  - lead_date               TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Lead creation date/time
  - lead_title              VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Optional lead title or subject
  - lead_name               VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Prospect/contact name
  - company_name            VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Lead organization name (B2B)
  - mobile_no               VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Primary mobile number
  - whatsapp_no             VARCHAR(30)     NULL      WhatsApp number
  - email                   VARCHAR(150)    NULL      Email address
  - lead_source_id          BIGINT          NULL      Source classification (link to biz.lead_source_master)
  - lead_status_id          BIGINT          NULL      Current status (link to biz.lead_status_master)
  - assigned_to_staff_id    BIGINT          NULL      Assigned salesperson/staff member
  - customer_id             BIGINT          NULL      Linked customer after conversion (nullable)
  - expected_value          NUMERIC(18,2)   NULL      Expected transaction value
  - probability_percent     NUMERIC(5,2)    NULL      Estimated probability (0-100%)
  - next_followup_at        TIMESTAMP       NULL      Next scheduled follow-up action
  - address                 VARCHAR(300)    NULL      Street address
  - city                    VARCHAR(100)    NULL      City name
  - country                 VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Country name
  - remarks                 TEXT            NULL      Additional notes and observations
  - converted_at            TIMESTAMP       NULL      Conversion to customer timestamp
  - lost_at                 TIMESTAMP       NULL      Lost/rejected timestamp
  - record_status           VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Row status: ACTIVE, CLOSED, LOST
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by              BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by             BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id           -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id            -> core.station_master or branch master (when finalized)
  - lead_source_id       -> biz.lead_source_master (when finalized)
  - lead_status_id       -> biz.lead_status_master (when finalized)
  - assigned_to_staff_id -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - customer_id          -> biz.customer_master (nullable; only after conversion)
  - created_by           -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by          -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_lead_master_company_lead_id   (company_id, lead_id)
  - uq_biz_lead_master_company_lead_code (company_id, lead_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; branch assignment for reporting, follow-up, and ownership)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_lead_master_company_id                   (company_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_branch_id                    (branch_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_company_branch               (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_lead_status_id               (company_id, lead_status_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_lead_source_id               (company_id, lead_source_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_assigned_to_staff_id         (company_id, assigned_to_staff_id)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_next_followup_at             (company_id, next_followup_at)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_mobile_no                    (company_id, mobile_no)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_email                        (company_id, email)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_record_status                (company_id, record_status)
  - idx_biz_lead_master_lead_date                    (company_id, lead_date)

Notes:
  - assigned_to_staff_id can later be enforced with foreign key to core.staff_master.
  - customer_id is nullable: not all leads convert to customers; some become opportunities or remain lost.
  - lead_source_id and lead_status_id link to master tables for classification and workflow.
  - record_status tracks lifecycle: ACTIVE (in-progress), CLOSED (converted or won), LOST (rejected/no-show).
  - next_followup_at supports task/reminder systems and activity dashboards.
  - converted_at and lost_at timestamps capture lifecycle events and sales cycle metrics.
  - Expected value and probability_percent enable pipeline forecasting similar to opportunity_stage_master.
  - Branch assignment enables regional sales team reporting, territory management, and lead routing.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# biz.opportunity_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Stores sales opportunities linked to customers or leads, used for proposal,
              negotiation, and conversion pipeline management. Tracks opportunity lifecycle
              from creation through stage progression to won or lost closure.

Legacy (MSSQL): New table (no direct legacy source)

Primary key: id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL

Business keys (unique per company):
  - opportunity_id
  - opportunity_code

Columns:
  - id                      BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal primary key
  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Operational branch ownership
  - opportunity_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business opportunity identifier
  - opportunity_code        VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Human-readable reference code
  - opportunity_date        TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Opportunity creation date/time
  - customer_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Linked customer
  - lead_id                 BIGINT          NULL      Source lead if converted from lead
  - opportunity_stage_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Current pipeline stage (link to biz.opportunity_stage_master)
  - opportunity_name        VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Opportunity title or project name
  - estimated_value         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Estimated transaction/deal value
  - probability_percent     NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Close probability (0-100%)
  - expected_close_date     DATE            NOT NULL  Forecasted closing date
  - assigned_to_staff_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Responsible salesperson/manager
  - source_reference        VARCHAR(100)    NULL      External reference or internal campaign code
  - remarks                 TEXT            NULL      Additional notes and context
  - status                  VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Status: OPEN, WON, LOST, CANCELLED
  - won_at                  TIMESTAMP       NULL      Won/closed timestamp (null if not won)
  - lost_at                 TIMESTAMP       NULL      Lost/rejected timestamp (null if not lost)
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Row creation timestamp
  - created_by              BIGINT          NULL      Creator staff/user reference
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modification timestamp
  - modified_by             BIGINT          NULL      Last modifier staff/user reference

Foreign keys:
  - company_id              -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id               -> core.station_master or branch master (when finalized)
  - customer_id             -> biz.customer_master (when finalized)
  - lead_id                 -> biz.lead_master (nullable; when finalized)
  - opportunity_stage_id    -> biz.opportunity_stage_master (when finalized)
  - assigned_to_staff_id    -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - created_by              -> core.staff_master (when finalized)
  - modified_by             -> core.staff_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_opportunity_master_company_opportunity_id   (company_id, opportunity_id)
  - uq_biz_opportunity_master_company_opportunity_code (company_id, opportunity_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; multi-company isolation)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; opportunity ownership and branch-level sales reporting)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_company_id              (company_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_branch_id               (branch_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_company_branch          (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_opportunity_stage_id    (company_id, opportunity_stage_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_customer_id             (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_lead_id                 (company_id, lead_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_assigned_to_staff_id    (company_id, assigned_to_staff_id)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_status                  (company_id, status)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_expected_close_date     (company_id, expected_close_date)
  - idx_biz_opportunity_master_probability             (company_id, probability_percent)

Notes:
  - Opportunity lifecycle: created in OPEN status -> progresses through opportunity_stage_master stages -> closes as WON or LOST.
  - estimated_value * probability_percent / 100 gives weighted pipeline value for forecasting dashboards.
  - lead_id is nullable: opportunities can originate from leads or be created directly from customer requirements.
  - Branch assignment enables regional sales team reporting, territory management, and location-specific revenue tracking.
  - Can later link to quotation, proposal, and order tables to form complete sales-to-cash workflow.
  - status progression: OPEN (active) -> WON (closed successfully) or LOST (closed unsuccessfully) or CANCELLED (discarded).
  - won_at and lost_at timestamps enable sales cycle duration analysis and time-to-conversion metrics.
  - opportunity_code provides stable external reference for integrations and reporting systems.
  - Supports weighted pipeline forecasting: stages with higher probability_percent contribute more to revenue forecast.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# biz.product_substitute
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Company-level substitute mapping: which unique product can replace another,
              with audit and record lifecycle — business rule table (not branch-scoped).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.SubstituteMaster (identity ID; no explicit PK on SubstituteID in source).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - product_substitute_id (legacy SubstituteID)
  - (company_id, product_unique_id, substitute_product_unique_id) — one row per directed pair

Columns:
  - id                             BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - product_substitute_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business mapping id (legacy SubstituteID)
  - company_id                     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier (new)
  - product_unique_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Main product unique id (legacy UniqueProductID)
  - substitute_product_unique_id   BIGINT          NOT NULL  Substitute unique id (legacy SubstituteUniqueProductID)
  - created_by                     VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - created_on                     TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - modified_by                    VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)
  - modified_on                    TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - record_status                  VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (new; ACTIVE/INACTIVE/DELETED)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - (company_id, product_unique_id) and (company_id, substitute_product_unique_id) -> core.product_master or
    core.product_inventory unique-key column when product design is finalized (deferred).

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_product_substitute_company_substitute_id  (company_id, product_substitute_id)
  - uq_biz_product_substitute_company_pair             (company_id, product_unique_id, substitute_product_unique_id)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - product_unique_id <> substitute_product_unique_id
  - record_status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','DELETED')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-wide mapping)
  station_id: Not included

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_product_substitute_company_product        (company_id, product_unique_id)
  - idx_biz_product_substitute_company_substitute     (company_id, substitute_product_unique_id)
  - idx_biz_product_substitute_company_status         (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - SubstituteMaster -> biz.product_substitute; id BIGSERIAL PK + product_substitute_id; company_id + record_status added;
    unique pair prevents duplicate mappings; CHECK blocks self-substitute; FKs deferred until unique-product ownership
    is finalized on product master/inventory; blind defaults not carried unless you adopt DDL DEFAULTs.

# biz.project_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Project header: customer, owner staff, status, priority, timeline,
              budget/currency, location/contacts, multi-branch upload flags, sync/server/
              record lifecycle — business project management.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.ProjectMaster (PK ProjectID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - project_id — unique per company (legacy ProjectID)
  - (company_id, branch_id, project_no) — unique project number per branch

Columns:
  - id                      BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - project_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business project id (legacy ProjectID)
  - branch_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location (legacy StationID)
  - project_no              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Visible project no. (legacy ProjectNo)
  - project_name            VARCHAR(200)    NOT NULL  Title (legacy ProjectName)
  - project_location        VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Site/location (legacy Location)
  - contact_person          VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Contact name (legacy ContactPerson)
  - contact_no              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Phone (legacy ContactNo)
  - customer_id             BIGINT          NULL      Client (legacy CustomerID)
  - owner_staff_id          BIGINT          NULL      Owner/responsible staff (legacy OwnerStaffID)
  - project_status          VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. ACTIVE, CLOSED (legacy Status)
  - priority                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Priority (legacy Priority)
  - start_date              TIMESTAMP       NULL      Start (legacy StartDate)
  - end_date                TIMESTAMP       NULL      End (legacy EndDate)
  - expected_hours          NUMERIC(9,2)    NOT NULL  Estimated hours (legacy ExpectedHours)
  - budget_amount           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Budget (legacy Budget)
  - currency_code           VARCHAR(10)     NULL      Budget currency (legacy Currency)
  - remarks                 VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remarks)
  - upload_to_multi_branch  BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Share across branches (legacy UploadToMultiStation)
  - sync_status             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatusC)
  - server_status           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Server state (legacy ServerStatusC)
  - record_status           VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (legacy RStatusC)
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - (company_id, customer_id) -> biz.customer_master when enforced; owner_staff_id -> staff master;
    branch_id -> branch master; currency_code -> core.currency_master (optional).

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_project_master_company_project_id       (company_id, project_id)
  - uq_biz_project_master_company_branch_project_no (company_id, branch_id, project_no)

Constraints (check) (suggested):
  - chk_biz_project_master_dates           end_date IS NULL OR start_date IS NULL OR end_date >= start_date
  - chk_biz_project_master_budget_nonneg  budget_amount >= 0
  - chk_biz_project_master_hours_nonneg   expected_hours >= 0

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_branch              (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_customer            (company_id, customer_id)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_owner_staff         (company_id, owner_staff_id)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_project_status      (company_id, project_status)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_priority            (company_id, priority)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_branch_status       (company_id, branch_id, project_status)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_start_date          (company_id, start_date)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_end_date            (company_id, end_date)
  - idx_biz_project_master_company_record_status       (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - Business-domain projects in biz schema; StationID -> branch_id; Currency -> currency_code; project_name required;
    project_no unique per company+branch; validate dates and non-negative budget/hours in DDL.

  - po_box                 VARCHAR(15)     NULL      PO Box (legacy POBox)
  - city                   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      City (legacy City)
  - country                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Country (legacy Country)
  - telephone              VARCHAR(25)     NULL      Phone (legacy TelePhone)
  - fax_no                 VARCHAR(15)     NULL      Fax (legacy FaxNo)
  - email                  VARCHAR(75)     NULL      Email (legacy Email)
  - contact_person         VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Contact (legacy ContactPerson)
  - mobile_no              VARCHAR(25)     NULL      Mobile (legacy MobileNo)
  - payment_mode           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Default payment mode (legacy PaymentMode)
  - credit_balance         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Outstanding balance (legacy CreditBalance)
  - credit_limit           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Credit limit (legacy CreditLimit)
  - credit_period          INTEGER         NOT NULL  Credit period days (legacy CreditPeriod; money -> INTEGER)
  - remarks                VARCHAR(750)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remarks)
  - supplier_tax_reg_no    VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Tax/VAT reg (legacy SupplierTaxRegNo)
  - created_by             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - created_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - modified_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)
  - modified_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - sync_status            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - record_status          VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (legacy RStatus)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_biz_supplier_master_company_supplier_id          (company_id, supplier_id)
  - uq_biz_supplier_master_company_supplier_code        (company_id, supplier_code)
  - uq_biz_supplier_master_company_supplier_no_numeric  (company_id, supplier_no_numeric)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - credit_balance >= 0; credit_limit >= 0; credit_period >= 0

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (company-wide supplier master)
  station_id: Removed from redesign (legacy StationID)

Indexes:
  - idx_biz_supplier_master_company_supplier_name    (company_id, supplier_name)
  - idx_biz_supplier_master_company_contact_person   (company_id, contact_person)
  - idx_biz_supplier_master_company_mobile_no        (company_id, mobile_no)
  - idx_biz_supplier_master_company_email            (company_id, email)
  - idx_biz_supplier_master_company_sync_status      (company_id, sync_status)
  - idx_biz_supplier_master_company_record_status    (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - SupplierMaster -> biz.supplier_master; id BIGSERIAL + supplier_id; company_id added; StationID dropped;
    UploadStatus -> sync_status; RStatus -> record_status; CreditPeriod as INTEGER days; removed bad MSSQL defaults;
    if many rows have supplier_no_numeric NULL, replace UNIQUE(company_id, supplier_no_numeric) with a partial unique index
    WHERE supplier_no_numeric IS NOT NULL; snake_case.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# core.app_parameter
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Application/module configuration (report times, precision, barcode
              modes, feature flags, approvals, GRN modes, display, code behavior).
              Replaces legacy InvParameterTable with multi-company (+ optional branch) design.

Primary key: id

Business keys / uniqueness:
  - Logical uniqueness: (company_id, branch_id, parameter_key) with branch_id NULL
    meaning company-wide — implemented as a PostgreSQL unique index on
    (company_id, COALESCE(branch_id, 0), parameter_key):
    uq_app_parameter_company_branch_key

Columns:
  - id              BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - parameter_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business parameter id
  - company_id      BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id       BIGINT          NULL      Branch (NULL = company-level setting)
  - parameter_key   VARCHAR(100)    NOT NULL  System key e.g. report_start_time
  - parameter_name  VARCHAR(150)    NOT NULL  Display name
  - numeric_value   NUMERIC(18,4)   NULL      Numeric value
  - string_value    VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Text value
  - description     VARCHAR(1000)   NULL      Help / description
  - category        VARCHAR(100)    NULL      e.g. POS, Purchase, Report, Inventory
  - is_hidden       BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Hide in UI
  - is_active       BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active flag
  - created_on      TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - created_by      VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by
  - modified_on     TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Updated at
  - modified_by     VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Updated by

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id  -> branch master (when finalized)

Constraints / indexes:
  - uq_app_parameter_company_branch_key  UNIQUE (company_id, COALESCE(branch_id, 0), parameter_key)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_company_id    (company_id)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_branch_id     (branch_id)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_parameter_id  (parameter_id)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_parameter_key (parameter_key)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_category      (category)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_is_hidden     (is_hidden)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_is_active     (is_active)
  - idx_core_app_parameter_company_branch (company_id, branch_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Optional (NULL = company-wide; set = branch override)
  station_id: Not in redesign (use separate table for device/station settings if needed)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: InvParameterTable; PostgreSQL: core.app_parameter.
  - ParameterName split into parameter_key / parameter_name; NumericValue, Stringvalue -> numeric_value,
    string_value; IsHide -> is_hidden; added is_active; station_id removed.
  - Use for app/config only — not for structured masters (VAT groups, account heads, currencies,
    shifts, price levels as first-class entities belong in dedicated tables).

              payload per company and module as the persisted runtime override layer.

Columns:
  - id          BIGSERIAL    NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id  BIGINT       NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - module      VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL  Module code / namespace (default POS)
  - settings    JSONB        NOT NULL  Module settings JSON payload
  - updated_at  TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL  Last update timestamp
  - updated_by  VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL  Last updated by

Foreign keys:
  - None enforced in current live DB

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_company_parameter_value_company_module (company_id, module)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No

Indexes:
  - idx_company_parameter_value_company  (company_id)
  - idx_company_parameter_value_module   (company_id, module)

Notes:
  - Actual live DB table.
  - Stores a JSONB settings snapshot rather than one row per individual parameter.
  - Complements core.parameter_definition for default/metadata-driven settings.

  - price_level           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Optional price level (legacy PriceLevel)
  - sync_status           VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - server_status         VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Server state (legacy ServerStatus)
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:

  - idx_core_area_master_company_branch_supply_type        (company_id, branch_id, supply_type)
  - idx_core_area_master_company_branch_tablet_show        (company_id, branch_id, is_tablet_show)
  - idx_core_area_master_company_branch_sync_status        (company_id, branch_id, sync_status)

Notes:
  - Create before core.table_master when table_master.area_id references this table; StationID -> branch_id;
    UploadStatus -> sync_status; legacy numeric flags -> BOOLEAN where noted; removed bad defaults ('0', '1900-01-01');
    Arabic name kept per spec (consider TEXT if lengths exceed VARCHAR(50)); snake_case.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# core.software_type_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Central registry of all MOIFONE product lines / software modules.
              Used by company_master to identify which ERP module a company is
              licensed for. The frontend uses this to show or hide module-specific
              UI fields (e.g. Product Code is mandatory for Garage ERP but
              auto-generated for Restaurant / POS).

Primary key: id

Business key:
  - software_type_id  (unique numeric id per product line)
  - software_code     (unique short code e.g. RESTAURANT, POS, GARAGE)

Columns:
  - id                BIGSERIAL    NOT NULL  Internal surrogate PK
  - software_type_id  BIGINT       NOT NULL  Business key for this product line
  - software_code     VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL  Short uppercase code (RESTAURANT, POS, GARAGE, HR, CRM)
  - software_name     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL  Human-readable name shown in Moifone UI
  - description       VARCHAR(500) NULL      What this software covers
  - is_active         BOOLEAN      NOT NULL  Whether this product line is live (DEFAULT true)
  - display_order     SMALLINT     NOT NULL  Sort order in dropdowns (DEFAULT 0)
  - created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at        TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL  Row last modified

Seeded rows (migration 014):
  1  RESTAURANT  Restaurant ERP   - POS, kitchen, tables, delivery, recipe costing
  2  POS         ERP POS          - Grocery / supermarket / retail point-of-sale
  3  GARAGE      Garage ERP       - Job cards, OEM part codes, vehicle tracking
  4  HR          HR & Payroll     - Attendance, leave, payroll, employee self-service
  5  CRM         CRM              - Leads, pipeline, follow-ups, support tickets

Foreign keys:
  - None (referenced by core.company_master.software_type_id)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_software_type_master_type_id  (software_type_id)
  - uq_software_type_master_code     (software_code)
  - uq_software_type_master_name     (software_name)

Scope:
  company_id: No  (global lookup table shared across all companies)
  branch_id:  No

Indexes:
  - idx_software_type_master_active  (is_active)

Notes:
  - Added in migration 014_core_software_type.sql.
  - software_code values are used as constants in the frontend
    (e.g. company.softwareType === 'GARAGE' to show OEM part-code fields).
  - New product lines: INSERT a new row; no schema change needed.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# core.company_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Root company entity for multi-company ERP: identification, profile,
              ownership reference from other masters/transactions, active/inactive.
              Also carries software_type_id to identify which MOIFONE product line
              this company is running (Restaurant ERP, POS, Garage, HR, CRM).

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - company_id
  - company_code

Columns:
  - id                BIGSERIAL     NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id        BIGINT        NOT NULL  Business company id (this row's entity id)
  - company_code      VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  Company code
  - company_name      VARCHAR(200)  NOT NULL  Company name
  - company_address   VARCHAR(200)  NULL      Address
  - status            VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  e.g. ACTIVE / INACTIVE
  - contact_person    VARCHAR(100)  NULL      Main contact
  - phone             VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Contact number
  - software_type_id  BIGINT        NULL      FK -> core.software_type_master (added migration 014)
  - created_at        TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at        TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row last updated

Foreign keys:
  - software_type_id -> core.software_type_master (software_type_id)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_company_master_company_id   (company_id)
  - uq_company_master_company_code (company_code)
  - uq_company_master_company_name (company_name)

Scope:
  Entity table: company_id is the business identifier for the row (not a separate
  "owning company" column). branch_id: No (branches live in core.branch_master).

Indexes:
  - idx_core_company_master_status           (status)
  - idx_core_company_master_company_name     (company_name)
  - idx_core_company_master_software_type_id (software_type_id)  <- added migration 014

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: CompanyMaster; PostgreSQL: core.company_master.
  - MSSQL PK was CompanyID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + company_id.
  - CompanyCode: BIGINT -> VARCHAR(50) (code as identifier, not arithmetic).
  - CompanyID, CompanyName, CompanyAddress, ContactPerson, Phone -> snake_case.
  - StationID removed; branch/station structure via separate tables (e.g. branch_master).
  - Modular redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.
  - software_type_id added migration 014; existing companies back-filled to 1 (RESTAURANT).

  - parameter_name   VARCHAR(150)    NOT NULL  Display label (legacy ParameterName)

  - modified_at      TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (new)
  - modified_by      VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Modified by (new)

  - idx_core_counter_parameter_company_branch_counter        (company_id, branch_id, counter_id)
  - idx_core_counter_parameter_company_branch_counter_category (company_id, branch_id, counter_id, category)

Notes:

  - Legacy had no company/branch/counter ownership; parameter_key added for stable app logic vs display name only.
  - UploadStatus -> sync_status; do not carry blind MSSQL defaults like '0' / ' 0'; FKs when masters exist.

# core.document_reference_map
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Generic document-to-document links across modules (order form -> LPO,
              LPO -> GRN, KOT -> sales, material request -> transfer, invoice -> return).
              Central cross-reference mapping; no module-specific FKs.

Legacy (MSSQL): MultiReferenceTable (PK MultiTableID; identity ID).

Primary key: document_reference_id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL NOT NULL UNIQUE (surrogate; document_reference_id remains PK; legacy ID)

Columns:
  - document_reference_id  BIGINT          NOT NULL  PK — mapping id (legacy MultiTableID)
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal surrogate (unique; legacy ID)
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier (new in PG design)
  - branch_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch identifier (new in PG design)
  - source_doc_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Source document id (legacy TypeId)
  - source_doc_type        VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Source type e.g. LPO, KOT, SALES, GRN (legacy Type)
  - source_doc_no          VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Source document no. (legacy TypeNo)
  - reference_doc_id       BIGINT          NOT NULL  Linked document id (legacy RefID)
  - reference_doc_type     VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Linked type (legacy RefType)
  - reference_doc_no       VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Linked document no. (legacy RefNo)
  - sync_status            VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Upload/sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - record_status          VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Record state (legacy RStatus)

Foreign keys:
  - None intentionally — targets may live in different schemas/modules.

Constraints (unique):
  - id UNIQUE (non-PK surrogate)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_company_id             (company_id)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_branch_id              (branch_id)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_source_doc_id          (source_doc_id)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_source_doc_type        (source_doc_type)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_reference_doc_id       (reference_doc_id)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_reference_doc_type     (reference_doc_type)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_company_branch_source     (company_id, branch_id, source_doc_type, source_doc_id)
  - idx_core_document_reference_map_company_branch_reference  (company_id, branch_id, reference_doc_type, reference_doc_id)

Notes:
  - Shared cross-module reference data in core; clearer source/reference naming than legacy Type/Ref columns.
  - UploadStatus -> sync_status; RStatus -> record_status; snake_case; company_id and branch_id added for multi-company ERP.
  - Legacy column map: ID -> id; MultiTableID -> document_reference_id; TypeId/Type/TypeNo -> source_doc_*; RefID/RefType/RefNo -> reference_doc_*.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# core.document_sequence
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Running document/master numbering across the ERP (sales invoice, KOT,
              GRN, purchase, voucher, quotation, job card, product/supplier/customer
              masters). Replaces legacy IDControlManager with multi-company design.

Primary key: id

Business rule / unique key:
  - (company_id, branch_id, sequence_code) — one row per sequence scope
    (company-level: branch_id NULL; branch-level: branch_id set). For PostgreSQL,
    consider partial unique indexes if duplicate (company_id, NULL, code) rows must
    be prevented explicitly.

Columns:
  - id             BIGSERIAL     NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id     BIGINT        NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id      BIGINT        NULL      Branch (NULL = company-wide sequence)
  - sequence_code  VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  e.g. sales_invoice, grn, product_master
  - sequence_name  VARCHAR(100)  NOT NULL  Display name
  - current_value  BIGINT        NOT NULL  Last used number
  - step_value     INTEGER       NOT NULL  Increment step
  - prefix         VARCHAR(20)   NULL      Optional number prefix
  - suffix         VARCHAR(20)   NULL      Optional number suffix
  - is_active      BOOLEAN       NOT NULL  Active flag
  - created_on     TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Created at
  - created_by     VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Created by
  - modified_on    TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Updated at
  - modified_by    VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Updated by

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id  -> branch master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_core_document_sequence_company_branch_code (company_id, branch_id, sequence_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Optional (NULL = company-level sequence; set = branch-scoped sequence)

Indexes:
  - idx_core_document_sequence_company_id    (company_id)
  - idx_core_document_sequence_branch_id     (branch_id)
  - idx_core_document_sequence_sequence_code (sequence_code)
  - idx_core_document_sequence_is_active     (is_active)
  - idx_core_document_sequence_company_branch (company_id, branch_id)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: IDControlManager; PostgreSQL: core.document_sequence.
  - ControlName -> sequence_code; ControlValue -> current_value; ControlStep -> step_value;
    added prefix, suffix, is_active; multi-company + nullable branch_id for both numbering models.
  - For business document numbers only — not internal row IDs (use BIGSERIAL/identity on tables).
  - Generate next values inside a transaction with row lock (e.g. SELECT ... FOR UPDATE) under concurrency.

# core.product_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Central product/item catalog for POS, inventory, purchase, sales,
              reporting, garage spares, and related modules.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.ProductMaster (PK ProductID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - product_id
  - product_code (new clean ERP code; generated/mapped at migration)

Columns:
  - id                         BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - product_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business product id (legacy ProductID)
  - product_code               VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  ERP product code (new)
  - barcode                    VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Barcode (legacy BarCode)
  - own_ref_no                 NUMERIC(18,0)   NULL      Internal ref no. (legacy ProductOwnRefNo)
  - product_name               VARCHAR(150)    NOT NULL  Main name (legacy Description)
  - short_name                 VARCHAR(150)    NULL      Short name (legacy ShortDescription)
  - specification              VARCHAR(250)    NULL      Spec/technical detail (legacy Specification)
  - description_arabic         VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Arabic (legacy DescriptionArabic)
  - brand_id                   BIGINT          NULL      Brand ref (legacy ProductBrand)
  - product_type               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Classification (legacy ProductType)
  - make_type                  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Make/type (legacy ProductMakeType)
  - group_id                   BIGINT          NULL      Main group (legacy GroupID)
  - subgroup_id                BIGINT          NULL      Sub-group (legacy SubGroupID)
  - subsubgroup_id             BIGINT          NULL      Sub-sub-group (legacy SubSubGroupID)
  - unit_name                  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      UOM name (legacy Unit)
  - pack_qty                   NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Pack qty (legacy PackQty)
  - pack_description           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Pack text (legacy PacketDescription)
  - barcode_type               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Barcode format (legacy BarCodeType)
  - is_master_product          BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Base/master product (legacy IsMaster)
  - stock_type                 VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Stock class (legacy StockType)
  - remarks                    VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remarks)
  - product_status             VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. ACTIVE/INACTIVE (legacy ProductStatus)
  - last_supplier_id           BIGINT          NULL      Last supplier (legacy LastSupplierID)
  - unique_multi_product_id    BIGINT          NULL      Multi-product/variant link (legacy UniqueMultiProductID)
  - sync_status                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - substitute_product_id      BIGINT          NULL      Substitute product (legacy SubstituteNo)
  - model_no                   INTEGER         NULL      Model ref (legacy Model)
  - engine_no                  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Engine no. (legacy EngineNo)
  - chassis_no                 VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Chassis (legacy ChassieNo; spelling fixed)
  - oem_code                   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      OEM code (legacy OEMCode)
  - parts_family               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Parts family (legacy PartsFamily)
  - is_daily_transaction_item  BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Daily-use item flag (legacy DailyTransactionItem)
  - expiry_date                TIMESTAMP       NULL      Expiry when relevant (legacy ExpiryDate)
  - cooking_time_minutes       NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Prep time (legacy CoockingTime; spelling fixed)
  - parcel_charges             NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Parcel charge (legacy ParcelCharges)
  - additional_charges         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Extra charge (legacy AddCharges)
  - kitchen_location_id        BIGINT          NULL      Kitchen routing (legacy KitchenLocationID)
  - image_url                  VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Image URL/path (legacy ImageLocation)
  - cooking_notes              VARCHAR(150)    NULL      Kitchen notes (legacy CoockingNotes)
  - counter_popup_flag         BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Counter popup (legacy CounterPOPUP)
  - server_status              VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Server state (legacy ServerStatus)
  - record_status              VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Row lifecycle (legacy RStatusM)
  - product_identity           BIGINT          NULL      Legacy identity ref (legacy ProductIdentity)
  - priority_id                BIGINT          NULL      Priority ref (legacy PriorityID)
  - created_at                 TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by                 VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at                TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys:
  - Deferred until masters stable: brand_id, group_id, subgroup_id, subsubgroup_id, last_supplier_id,
    substitute_product_id, kitchen_location_id, priority_id -> respective tables; self-FK for substitute optional.

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_core_product_master_company_product_id   (company_id, product_id)
  - uq_core_product_master_company_product_code (company_id, product_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No (company-level catalog; branch stock/price/reorder/tax in child/inventory tables)

Indexes:
  - idx_core_product_master_company_product_status          (company_id, product_status)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_barcode                 (company_id, barcode)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_short_name              (company_id, short_name)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_group_id                 (company_id, group_id)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_subgroup_id              (company_id, subgroup_id)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_brand_id                 (company_id, brand_id)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_unique_multi_product_id  (company_id, unique_multi_product_id)
  - idx_core_product_master_company_record_status            (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - MSSQL redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK; product_id + new product_code both unique per company; StationID dropped from master.
  - ImageLocation -> image_url for cloud/CDN; Coocking* -> cooking_*; blind text/date defaults like '0' not carried forward;
    FKs when related masters are stable.

# core.product_inventory
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Branch-level stock, reorder, pricing, margins, discounts, and tax for
              each product — extends core.product_master with per-branch operational
              data (legacy ProductChild).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.ProductChild (PK ProductChildID + identity ID).

  - product_inventory_id — unique per company (legacy ProductChildID)
  - (company_id, branch_id, product_id) — one inventory row per product per branch

  - company_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company (new)
  - product_inventory_id       BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business inventory row id (legacy ProductChildID)
  - product_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent product (legacy ProductID)
  - branch_id                  BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch/station (legacy StationID)
  - unique_multi_product_id    BIGINT          NULL      Multi-product ref (legacy UniqueMultiProductID)
  - pack_qty                   NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Pack qty (legacy PackQty)
  - qty_on_hand                NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Stock on hand (legacy QtyOnHand)
  - reorder_level              NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Reorder threshold (legacy ReOrderLevel)
  - reorder_qty                NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Suggested reorder qty (legacy ReOrderQty)
  - correction_factor          NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Stock/price correction (legacy CorrectionFactor)
  - last_purchase_cost         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Last purchase cost (legacy LastPurchaseCost)
  - average_cost               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Average cost (legacy AverageCost)
  - unit_price                 NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Selling price (legacy UnitPrice)
  - minimum_retail_price       NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Min retail (legacy MinimumRetailPrice)
  - maximum_retail_price       NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Max retail (legacy MaxRetailPrice)
  - price_level_1              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Price level 1 (legacy PriceLevel1)
  - price_level_2              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Price level 2 (legacy PriceLevel2)
  - price_level_3              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Price level 3 (legacy PriceLevel3)
  - price_level_4              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Price level 4 (legacy PriceLevel4)
  - price_level_5              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Price level 5 (legacy PriceLevel5)
  - location_code              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Bin/rack code (legacy Location)
  - margin_amount              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Margin amount (legacy Margin)
  - minimum_margin_percentage  NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Min margin % (legacy MinimumMarginPercentage)
  - discount_percentage        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Discount % (legacy DiscountPercentage)
  - discount_date_from         TIMESTAMP       NULL      Discount start (legacy DiscountDateFrom)
  - discount_date_to           TIMESTAMP       NULL      Discount end (legacy DiscountDateTo)
  - input_tax_1_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Input tax 1 amount (legacy InputTax1Amount)
  - input_tax_2_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Input tax 2 amount (legacy InputTax2Amount)
  - input_tax_3_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Input tax 3 amount (legacy InputTax3Amount)
  - input_tax_1_rate           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Input tax 1 rate (legacy InputTax1Rate)
  - input_tax_2_rate           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Input tax 2 rate (legacy InputTax2Rate)
  - input_tax_3_rate           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Input tax 3 rate (legacy InputTax3Rate)
  - output_tax_1_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Output tax 1 amount (legacy Tax1Amount)
  - output_tax_2_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Output tax 2 amount (legacy Tax2Amount)
  - output_tax_3_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Output tax 3 amount (legacy Tax3Amount)
  - output_tax_1_rate          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Output tax 1 rate (legacy Tax1Rate)
  - output_tax_2_rate          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Output tax 2 rate (legacy Tax2Rate)
  - output_tax_3_rate          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Output tax 3 rate (legacy Tax3Rate)
  - sync_status                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - upload_to_multi_branch     VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Multi-branch upload flag (legacy UploadToMultiStation)
  - server_status              VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Server state (legacy ServerStatusC)
  - record_status              VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Row lifecycle (legacy RStatusC)
  - created_at                 TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by                 VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at                TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys:
  - (company_id, product_id) -> core.product_master(company_id, product_id) (recommended when enforced)
  - branch_id -> branch master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_core_product_inventory_company_business_id     (company_id, product_inventory_id)
  - uq_core_product_inventory_company_branch_product  (company_id, branch_id, product_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_branch                    (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_product_id                (company_id, product_id)
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_branch_qty_on_hand        (company_id, branch_id, qty_on_hand)
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_branch_reorder            (company_id, branch_id, reorder_level, reorder_qty)
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_branch_unit_price         (company_id, branch_id, unit_price)
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_branch_record_status      (company_id, branch_id, record_status)
  - idx_core_product_inventory_company_unique_multi_product_id   (company_id, unique_multi_product_id)

Notes:
  - ProductChild renamed to product_inventory; StationID -> branch_id; Tax*Amount/Rate -> output_tax_*; input tax names kept;
    placeholder MSSQL defaults ('0', 1/1/1900) not carried — use NULL dates / numeric defaults in DDL.
  - Complements company-level core.product_master for branch stock, pricing, and tax.

  - sync_status      VARCHAR(50)     NULL*     Sync (legacy UploadStatus). *Some DDL uses NOT NULL;
                                              Moifone self-registration API inserts 'PENDING'.
  - server_status    VARCHAR(50)     NULL*     Server state (legacy ServerStatus). *Same as sync_status;
                                              API inserts 'PENDING' for new web-registered staff.
  - record_status    VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle e.g. ACTIVE/INACTIVE (new standard)
  - created_at       TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by       VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at      TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

  - idx_core_staff_master_company_role_id       (company_id, role_id)
  - idx_core_staff_master_company_duty_status   (company_id, duty_status)
  - idx_core_staff_master_company_record_status (company_id, record_status)
  - idx_core_staff_master_company_server_status (company_id, server_status)

Notes:
  - Core staff master; StationID -> branch_id; SystemRoleID -> role_id; Password -> password_hash (migrate to strong hashing, never plaintext);

  - report_footer_image   BYTEA         NULL      Report footer image

  - bank_code             VARCHAR(100)  NULL      Bank code
  - van_no                VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Van number
  - delivery_boy_id       BIGINT        NULL      Delivery boy id
  - salesman_id           BIGINT        NULL      Salesman id
  - driver_id             BIGINT        NULL      Driver id
  - port0                 VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Port setting
  - port1                 VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Port setting
  - port2                 VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Port setting
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Created by (legacy cr_by)
  - created_on            TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Created at (legacy cr_on)
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Modified by (legacy mod_by)
  - modified_on           TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy mod_on)

  - salesman_id      -> HR/staff master (when finalized)
  - driver_id        -> HR/staff master (when finalized)

  - uq_core_station_master_company_station_code (company_id, station_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (omitted until company/branch/station hierarchy is finalized)

  - idx_core_station_master_salesman_id     (salesman_id)
  - idx_core_station_master_driver_id       (driver_id)

  - MSSQL PK was StationID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + station_id; added company_id; branch_id not added yet.
  - varbinary(max) -> BYTEA for report images; SalesManID -> salesman_id.
  - Represents an operating location/station, not only a device record.

  - table_name_arabic     VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Arabic name (legacy TableNameArabic)
  - assigned_waiter_id    BIGINT          NULL      Waiter/staff (legacy WaiterID)
  - no_of_chairs          INTEGER         NOT NULL  Seating capacity (legacy NoOfChairs; default 1 in app/DDL)
  - table_format          VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Shape/visual (legacy TableFormat; default SQUARE in DDL)
  - sync_status           VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - server_status         VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Server state (legacy ServerStatus)
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

  - uq_core_table_master_company_branch_area_table_name  (company_id, branch_id, area_id, table_name)

  - no_of_chairs > 0
  - table_format IN ('SQUARE','ROUND','RECTANGLE','CUSTOM')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_core_table_master_company_branch                 (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_core_table_master_company_branch_area            (company_id, branch_id, area_id)
  - idx_core_table_master_company_branch_waiter          (company_id, branch_id, assigned_waiter_id)
  - idx_core_table_master_company_branch_table_no        (company_id, branch_id, table_no)

# garage.car_sub_group
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  - company_id           BIGINT        NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - car_sub_group_id     BIGINT        NOT NULL  Business car sub-group id
  - car_group_id         BIGINT        NOT NULL  Parent car group id
  - car_sub_group_name   VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  Sub-group name
  - upload_status        VARCHAR(50)   NULL      Legacy sync/upload status
  - created_at           TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at           TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row last updated

Foreign keys:
  - fk_car_sub_group_car_group: (company_id, car_group_id)
    -> garage.car_group (company_id, car_group_id)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_car_sub_group_company_sub_group_id        (company_id, car_sub_group_id)
  - uq_car_sub_group_company_group_sub_group_name (company_id, car_group_id, car_sub_group_name)
    (same name allowed in different groups; no duplicate name within one group)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (classification master, not branch transactional data)

Indexes:
  - idx_garage_car_sub_group_company_id      (company_id)
  - idx_garage_car_sub_group_car_group_id    (car_group_id)
  - idx_garage_car_sub_group_name            (car_sub_group_name)
  - idx_garage_car_sub_group_upload_status   (upload_status)
  - idx_garage_car_sub_group_company_group   (company_id, car_group_id)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: CarSubGroup; PostgreSQL: garage.car_sub_group.
  - MSSQL had no car_group_id; redesign adds it for parent-child hierarchy.
  - Adds id BIGSERIAL PK, company_id, car_group_id, timestamps.
  - CarSubGroup -> car_sub_group_name; UploadStatus -> upload_status.
  - Normalized redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.

  - workshop_id  -> garage workshop master

  branch_id:  Yes (required)
  workshop_id: Operational field (separate from branch)

  - idx_garage_job_card_child_branch_id         (branch_id)
  - idx_garage_job_card_child_job_card_id       (job_card_id)

# garage.time_punching_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Technician time-punch transaction header: technician, job refs, time in/out,
              worked vs allocated minutes, punching type, workshop, punch status, sync —
              branch-scoped garage labor tracking.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.TimePunchingMaster (PK TimePunchingID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business key (unique per company and branch):
  - time_punching_id (legacy TimePunchingID)

Columns:
  - id                        BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id                BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - branch_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch/workshop location (new)
  - time_punching_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business punch header id (legacy TimePunchingID)
  - technician_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Technician (legacy TechnicianID)
  - job_no                    VARCHAR(15)     NULL      Job no. (legacy JobNo)
  - job_code                  VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Job code (legacy JobCode)
  - time_in                   TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Punch start (legacy TimeIn)
  - time_out                  TIMESTAMP       NULL      Punch end (legacy TimeOut; NULL while OPEN)
  - time_worked_minutes       BIGINT          NOT NULL  Worked minutes (legacy TimeWorked)
  - allocated_time_minutes    NUMERIC(18,2)   NULL      Planned minutes (legacy AllocatedTime)
  - punching_type_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Type ref (legacy PunchingType -> punching_type_id)
  - remarks                   VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remarks)
  - workshop_id               BIGINT          NULL      Workshop (legacy WorkshopID)
  - punch_status              VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  OPEN / CLOSED / CANCELLED / POSTED (legacy PunchStatus)
  - sync_status               VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - created_at                TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at               TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - (company_id, punching_type_id) -> garage.time_punching_type_master(company_id, time_punching_type_id) when enforced
  - (company_id, branch_id, technician_id) -> garage.technician_master(company_id, branch_id, technician_id) when enforced
  - workshop_id -> workshop master (when modeled)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch_id  (company_id, branch_id, time_punching_id)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - time_worked_minutes >= 0
  - allocated_time_minutes IS NULL OR allocated_time_minutes >= 0
  - punch_status IN ('OPEN','CLOSED','CANCELLED','POSTED')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch                  (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch_technician      (company_id, branch_id, technician_id)
  - idx_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch_job_no          (company_id, branch_id, job_no)
  - idx_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch_status          (company_id, branch_id, punch_status)
  - idx_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch_time_in         (company_id, branch_id, time_in)
  - idx_garage_time_punching_master_company_branch_sync_status     (company_id, branch_id, sync_status)

Notes:
  - Labor punching header in garage; UploadStatus -> sync_status; company_id + branch_id; TimeWorked -> time_worked_minutes;
    AllocatedTime -> allocated_time_minutes; PunchingType -> punching_type_id (FK to preceding time_punching_type_master);
    removed bad defaults; job_no/job_code string widths per spec — widen if legacy data exceeds; snake_case.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  - (company_id, branch_id, registration_no)

Business keys (unique per company):
  - chassis_no (legacy ChassisNo)
  - engine_no (legacy EngineNo)

Columns:
  - id                    BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - branch_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch/workshop (legacy StationCode)
  - vehicle_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business vehicle id (legacy VehicleID)
  - vehicle_brand_id      BIGINT          NOT NULL  Brand (legacy VehicleBrandID)
  - vehicle_type_id       BIGINT          NULL      Type (legacy VehicleTypeID)
  - model                 VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Model / variant (legacy Model; widened)
  - registration_no       VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Reg no. (legacy RegNo)
  - emirate               VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Emirate/region (legacy Emirate)
  - plate_colour          VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Plate colour (legacy PlateColour)
  - body_colour           VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Body colour (legacy BodyColour)
  - chassis_no            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Chassis (legacy ChassisNo)
  - engine_no             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Engine (legacy EngineNo)
  - plate_code            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Plate code (legacy PlateCode)
  - registration_date     TIMESTAMP       NULL      Reg date (legacy RegistrationDate)
  - expiry_date           TIMESTAMP       NULL      Expiry (legacy ExpiryDate)
  - door_key              VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Door key ref (legacy DoorKey)
  - remarks               VARCHAR(1500)   NULL      Notes (legacy Remarks)
  - purchase_invoice_no   VARCHAR(15)     NULL      Purchase inv (legacy PurchaseInvoiceNo)
  - purchase_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Purchase amt (legacy PurchaseAmount)
  - purchase_date         TIMESTAMP       NULL      Purchase date (legacy PurchaseDate)
  - insurance_no          VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Policy no. (legacy InsuranceNo)
  - insurance_company     VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Insurer (legacy InsuranceCompany)
  - insurance_amount      NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Insurance amt (legacy InsuranceAmount)
  - vehicle_status        VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  Lifecycle (legacy VehicleStatus)
  - warranty_policy       VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Warranty text (legacy WarrantyPolicy)
  - car_category          VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Category (legacy CarCategory)
  - serial_no             BIGINT          NULL      Serial ref (legacy SerialNo)
  - car_group_id          BIGINT          NULL      Car group (legacy CarGroupID)
  - car_subgroup_id       BIGINT          NULL      Car subgroup (legacy CarSubGroupID)
  - warranty_km           NUMERIC(18,0)   NOT NULL  Warranty km (legacy WarrantyKM)
  - image_url             TEXT            NULL      Main image URL (new; replaces binary / VehicleImageMaster)
  - sync_status           VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys:
  - (company_id, vehicle_brand_id) -> garage.vehicle_brand_master(company_id, vehicle_brand_id) (recommended when enforced)
  - (company_id, vehicle_type_id) -> garage.vehicle_type_master(company_id, vehicle_type_id) (recommended when enforced; nullable)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - car_group_id -> garage.car_group(company_id, car_group_id); car_subgroup_id -> garage.car_sub_group when keys align
  - branch_id -> branch / station master when finalized

  - uq_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_vehicle_id       (company_id, branch_id, vehicle_id)
  - uq_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_registration_no  (company_id, branch_id, registration_no)
  - uq_garage_vehicle_master_company_chassis_no              (company_id, chassis_no)
  - uq_garage_vehicle_master_company_engine_no               (company_id, engine_no)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - purchase_amount >= 0; insurance_amount >= 0; warranty_km >= 0
  - vehicle_status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','SOLD','SCRAPPED','IN_SERVICE')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch                    (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_brand              (company_id, branch_id, vehicle_brand_id)
  - idx_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_status             (company_id, branch_id, vehicle_status)
  - idx_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_registration_date  (company_id, branch_id, registration_date)
  - idx_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_expiry_date        (company_id, branch_id, expiry_date)
  - idx_garage_vehicle_master_company_branch_sync_status        (company_id, branch_id, sync_status)

Notes:
  - VehicleMaster -> garage.vehicle_master; StationCode -> branch_id; UploadStatus -> sync_status; money -> NUMERIC(18,2);
    image_url replaces DB binary / VehicleImageMaster; model widened; remarks long for history; FK to vehicle_brand_master and vehicle_type_master;
    UNIQUE on chassis/engine is company-scoped — use partial unique indexes WHERE chassis_no/engine_no IS NOT NULL if legacy has blanks;
    car_sub_group FK naming aligns with garage.car_sub_group; snake_case.

# ops.agent_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Agent master for operational flows (sales, purchase, commission):
              identification, commission %, branch-wise use, active/inactive,
              audit and sync tracking.

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - agent_id
  - agent_code

Columns:
  - id                       BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id                BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch identifier (legacy StationID)
  - agent_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business agent identifier
  - agent_code               VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Agent code
  - agent_name               VARCHAR(500)    NOT NULL  Agent name
  - mobile_no                VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Mobile number
  - commission_percentage    NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Commission %
  - created_by               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by user
  - created_on               TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Last modified by
  - modified_on              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modified at
  - upload_status            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Sync/upload status (legacy/integration)
  - active_status            VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Usually ACTIVE / INACTIVE

Foreign keys:
  - company_id -> company master (when finalized)
  - branch_id  -> branch master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_agent_master_company_agent_id   (company_id, agent_id)
  - uq_agent_master_company_agent_code (company_id, agent_code)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; legacy StationID normalized for branch ownership/filtering)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_agent_master_company_id           (company_id)
  - idx_ops_agent_master_branch_id            (branch_id)
  - idx_ops_agent_master_agent_name           (agent_name)
  - idx_ops_agent_master_active_status        (active_status)
  - idx_ops_agent_master_company_branch       (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_ops_agent_master_company_branch_active (company_id, branch_id, active_status)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: AgentMaster; PostgreSQL: ops.agent_master.
  - MSSQL PK was AgentID; redesign uses id BIGSERIAL PK plus company_id, branch_id.
  - MobileNo: NUMERIC -> VARCHAR(20); CommissionPercentage: money -> NUMERIC(18,2).
  - Audit: CrBy/CrOn/ModBy/ModOn -> created_by, created_on, modified_by, modified_on.
  - UploadStatus -> upload_status; ActiveStatus -> active_status.

  - description           VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Short description
  - master_product_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Linked master product id
  - server_status         VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Legacy server/sync status
  - record_status         VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. ACTIVE / INACTIVE
  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by user
  - created_on            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Last modified by
  - modified_on           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Last modified at

Foreign keys:
  - company_id         -> company master (when finalized)
  - master_product_id  -> product master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_alternate_product_master_company_alt_product_id (company_id, alternate_product_id)
  - uq_alternate_product_master_company_alt_barcode  (company_id, alternate_barcode)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required; mappings isolated per company)
  branch_id:  No  (company-level master; legacy StationID dropped — not core for this table)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_alternate_product_master_company_id            (company_id)
  - idx_ops_alternate_product_master_master_product_id     (master_product_id)
  - idx_ops_alternate_product_master_record_status         (record_status)
  - idx_ops_alternate_product_master_company_master_product (company_id, master_product_id)
  - idx_ops_alternate_product_master_company_record_status  (company_id, record_status)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: AlternateProductMaster; PostgreSQL: ops.alternate_product_master.
  - MSSQL PK was AlternateProductID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + company_id.
  - ServerStaturs -> server_status; RStatus -> record_status.
  - Audit: CrBy/CrOn/ModBy/ModOn -> created_by, created_on, modified_by, modified_on.
  - Modular redesign, not a direct MSSQL copy.

# ops.combo_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Combo / bundled product composition: child lines under a master
              product with qty and display order (meal/package, POS combo,
              composite configuration).

Primary key: id

Business rule:
  - Each child product appears at most once per (company, master_product)
    (enforced by unique constraint below).

Columns:
  - id                 BIGSERIAL     NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id         BIGINT        NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - master_product_id  BIGINT        NOT NULL  Parent / combo product id
  - child_product_id   BIGINT        NOT NULL  Child line product id
  - qty                INTEGER       NOT NULL  Quantity in the combo
  - display_num        INTEGER       NOT NULL  Order/sequence in the combo
  - created_at         TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row created
  - updated_at         TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL  Row last updated

Foreign keys:
  - company_id        -> company master (when finalized)
  - master_product_id -> product master (when finalized)
  - child_product_id  -> product master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_combo_master_company_master_child (company_id, master_product_id, child_product_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No  (composition master; branch-specific combos via separate design if needed)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_combo_master_company_id           (company_id)
  - idx_ops_combo_master_master_product_id    (master_product_id)
  - idx_ops_combo_master_child_product_id     (child_product_id)
  - idx_ops_combo_master_company_master_product (company_id, master_product_id)
  - idx_ops_combo_master_company_child_product  (company_id, child_product_id)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: ComboMaster; PostgreSQL: ops.combo_master.
  - Redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK, company_id, timestamps; ChildProductId, Qty, DisplayNum -> snake_case.
  - Removed legacy defaults such as '0'; company-level composition; branch_id omitted by design.
  - Modular redesign, not a blind MSSQL copy.

  - idx_ops_daily_counter_transaction_cashier_id            (cashier_id)
  - idx_ops_daily_counter_transaction_product_id            (product_id)
  - idx_ops_daily_counter_transaction_status                (status)
  - idx_ops_daily_counter_transaction_company_branch_date   (company_id, branch_id, issue_date)
  - idx_ops_daily_counter_transaction_company_branch_counter (company_id, branch_id, counter_no)
  - idx_ops_daily_counter_transaction_company_product       (company_id, product_id)

Notes:

  - Redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK, company_id, branch_id; StationID -> branch_id; CounterNo kept.
  - CoupenIssuedQty -> coupon_issued_qty; CounterCloseNO -> counter_close_no; Modby -> modified_by.

  - bill_no             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Bill number
  - bill_date           TIMESTAMP       NULL      Bill date
  - bill_time           TIMESTAMP       NULL      Bill time

  - kot_number          BIGINT          NULL      KOT number

  - server_status       VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Server status

  - sales_id          -> sales master
  - customer_id       -> biz.customer_master / customer master
  - product_id        -> product master
  - unique_product_id -> unique product table
  - group_id          -> group master (e.g. biz.group_master)
  - area_id           -> area master
  - table_id          -> dining table master
  - waiter_id         -> staff/employee master
  - deliveryboy_id    -> staff/employee master

Constraints (unique):

  - idx_ops_item_cancel_waiter_id                  (waiter_id)

  - idx_ops_item_cancel_server_status              (server_status)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: ItemClearTable; PostgreSQL: ops.item_clear.
  - StationID -> branch_id; money -> NUMERIC(18,2); Groupid -> group_id; removed defaults '0', '1/1/1900'.
  - No separate business unique key — event/audit style table.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ops.package_items
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Maps a package/combo product to its included line products: composition,
              display order, and reverse lookup (POS combo/bundle/meal kits, inventory,
              QR/menu).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.PackageItems (PK identity ID).

Primary key: id

Columns:
  - id                   BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier (new)
  - package_product_id   BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent package/combo product (legacy PackageProductID)
  - item_product_id      BIGINT          NOT NULL  Child product in package (legacy ItemProductID)
  - display_order        INTEGER         NOT NULL  Sort order under package (legacy DisplayOrder)
  - created_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CreatedOn)
  - created_by           VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Created by (new)
  - modified_at          TIMESTAMP       NULL      Last modified at (new)
  - modified_by          VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Modified by (new)

Foreign keys:
  - Deferred until final product master structure; package_product_id and item_product_id -> product master.

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_ops_package_items_company_package_item  UNIQUE (company_id, package_product_id, item_product_id)
    (replaces legacy unique on PackageProductID + ItemProductID only)

Constraints (check):
  - chk_ops_package_items_distinct_products  CHECK (package_product_id <> item_product_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  No (company-level catalog definition; use override table if branch-specific packages needed)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_package_items_company_id                 (company_id)
  - idx_ops_package_items_package_product_id         (package_product_id)
  - idx_ops_package_items_item_product_id            (item_product_id)
  - idx_ops_package_items_company_package_display    (company_id, package_product_id, display_order)

Notes:
  - Multi-company: unique includes company_id; simple MSSQL shape extended with audit and company scope.
  - FKs added after product master is finalized; structure-only — extend with qty/optional lines/pricing later if needed.

              printer, counter, per company+branch (Main Kitchen, Bar, Juice,

  - kitchen_location_id
  - kitchen_location_name (unique per company+branch — see constraints)

  - kitchen_location_id     BIGINT        NOT NULL  Business kitchen location id
  - company_id              BIGINT        NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id               BIGINT        NOT NULL  Branch identifier
  - kitchen_location_name   VARCHAR(50)   NOT NULL  Location name
  - printer_name            VARCHAR(100)  NULL      Printer for KOT routing (longer than legacy)
  - counter_no              BIGINT        NULL      Counter number

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id  -> branch master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):

  - uq_ops_kitchen_location_master_company_branch_name    (company_id, branch_id, kitchen_location_name)

  branch_id:  Yes (required; branch-specific printer routing)

  - MSSQL PK on ID; redesign: id surrogate PK + kitchen_location_id; added company_id, branch_id.
  - PrinterName -> printer_name VARCHAR(100); HMS/restaurant KOT workflows.
