# ops.kot_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Restaurant KOT header before final bill: numbering, status, counter,
              date/time, customer/delivery, dine-in (area/table/chair/waiter/salesman),
              tax/discount/subtotal/round-off, linked bill, dummy print, QR payment,
              remarks, audit.

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company):
  - kot_master_id

Columns:
  - id                        BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - kot_master_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business KOT header id
  - company_id                BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id                 BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch identifier (legacy StationID)
  - kot_number                BIGINT          NULL      KOT number
  - kot_prefix                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      KOT prefix
  - kot_status                VARCHAR(10)     NOT NULL  Status e.g. OPEN, HOLD
  - counter_no                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Counter number
  - kot_date                  TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  KOT date
  - kot_time                  TIMESTAMP       NULL      KOT time
  - customer_id               BIGINT          NULL      Customer id
  - delivery_boy_id           BIGINT          NULL      Delivery boy id
  - delivery_time             TIMESTAMP       NULL      Delivery time
  - area_id                   BIGINT          NULL      Dining area id
  - table_id                  BIGINT          NULL      Table id
  - chair_no                  BIGINT          NULL      Chair number
  - waiter_id                 BIGINT          NULL      Waiter id
  - salesman_id               BIGINT          NULL      Salesman id
  - upload_status_m           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Upload/sync status
  - bill_discount             NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Bill discount
  - amount                    NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Total amount
  - bill_id                   BIGINT          NULL      Linked bill id
  - created_by                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy cr_by)
  - created_on                TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy cr_on)
  - modified_by               VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy mod_by)
  - modified_on               TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy mod_on)
  - nof_customer              NUMERIC(18,0)   NOT NULL  Number of customers (legacy NofCustomer)
  - remarks                   VARCHAR(250)    NULL      Remarks
  - tax1_amount_m             NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 1 amount
  - tax2_amount_m             NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 2 amount
  - tax3_amount_m             NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 3 amount
  - tax1_rate_m               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 1 rate
  - tax2_rate_m               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 2 rate
  - tax3_rate_m               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 3 rate
  - sub_total_m               NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Subtotal
  - round_off_adj             NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Round-off adjustment
  - dummy_bill_print_status   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Dummy bill print status
  - qr_paid_amount            NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  QR paid amount
  - qr_balance_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  QR balance amount
  - qr_payment_status         VARCHAR(20)     NULL      QR payment status

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - company_id      -> core.company_master (when finalized)
  - branch_id       -> branch master (when finalized)
  - customer_id     -> customer master (when finalized)
  - delivery_boy_id -> staff/employee master (when finalized)
  - area_id         -> area master (when finalized)
  - table_id        -> dining table master (when finalized)
  - waiter_id       -> staff/employee master (when finalized)
  - salesman_id     -> staff/employee master (when finalized)
  - bill_id         -> sales/bill master (when finalized)

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_ops_kot_master_company_kot_master_id (company_id, kot_master_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)
  station_id: Not used (standardized to branch_id)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_kot_master_company_id               (company_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_branch_id                (branch_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_kot_number               (kot_number)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_kot_status                 (kot_status)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_counter_no                 (counter_no)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_kot_date                   (kot_date)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_customer_id                (customer_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_delivery_boy_id            (delivery_boy_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_area_id                    (area_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_table_id                   (table_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_waiter_id                  (waiter_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_salesman_id                (salesman_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_bill_id                    (bill_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_upload_status_m            (upload_status_m)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_dummy_bill_print_status    (dummy_bill_print_status)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_qr_payment_status          (qr_payment_status)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_company_branch_date        (company_id, branch_id, kot_date)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_company_branch_status      (company_id, branch_id, kot_status)
  - idx_ops_kot_master_company_branch_table       (company_id, branch_id, table_id)

Notes:
  - MSSQL original: KOTMaster; PostgreSQL: ops.kot_master.
  - MSSQL PK kotMasterID; redesign: id BIGSERIAL PK + kot_master_id; company_id, branch_id; StationID -> branch_id.
  - money -> NUMERIC(18,2); nvarchar(250) -> varchar(250); NofCustomer -> nof_customer; UploadStatusM -> upload_status_m.
  - QR fields kept for modern POS; kot_status VARCHAR for OPEN/HOLD-style values.

# ops.kot_child
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     KOT line items under ops.kot_master: product, qty, pricing, discount,
              tax, subtotal/line total, kitchen/Android print, sync/batch, audit.

Primary key: kot_child_id

Internal identity:
  - id BIGSERIAL NOT NULL UNIQUE (surrogate for technical/internal use; kot_child_id remains PK)

Columns:
  - kot_child_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  PK — business line id
  - id                  BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal surrogate (unique)
  - company_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Company identifier
  - branch_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch identifier
  - kot_master_id       BIGINT          NOT NULL  Parent KOT header id
  - product_id          BIGINT          NULL      Product id
  - barcode             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Barcode
  - unique_product_id   BIGINT          NULL      Unique / variant product id
  - short_description   VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Item description
  - description_arabic  VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Arabic description
  - qty                 NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Ordered qty
  - pack_qty            NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Pack qty
  - unit_cost           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Unit cost
  - unit_price          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Unit price
  - amount              NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Base line amount
  - item_discount       NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Line discount
  - sub_total           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Subtotal (per POS flow)
  - line_total          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Final line amount
  - tax_1_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 1 amount
  - tax_2_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 2 amount
  - tax_3_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 3 amount
  - tax_1_rate          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 1 rate
  - tax_2_rate          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 2 rate
  - tax_3_rate          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Tax 3 rate
  - group_id            BIGINT          NULL      Item group id
  - modifier            VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Modifier / addon text
  - android_printed     BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Android / KDS / kitchen print flag
  - print_count         INTEGER         NOT NULL  Print count
  - sync_status         VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Sync/upload status
  - kot_display_status  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Line display status
  - transaction_id      BIGINT          NULL      Linked billing/transaction id
  - batch_no            BIGINT          NULL      Batch grouping
  - batch_print_status  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Batch print status
  - created_by          VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by
  - created_at          TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by         VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by
  - modified_at         TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at

Foreign keys:
  - (company_id, kot_master_id) -> ops.kot_master(company_id, kot_master_id) (when enforced)
  - product_id, group_id -> add when product/group masters finalized (as noted in source spec)

Constraints (unique):
  - id UNIQUE (non-PK surrogate)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required; denormalized for filtering/reporting/sync)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_kot_child_company_id                 (company_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_branch_id                  (branch_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_kot_master_id              (kot_master_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_product_id                 (product_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_company_branch_master      (company_id, branch_id, kot_master_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_company_branch_product     (company_id, branch_id, product_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_android_printed            (company_id, branch_id, android_printed)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_transaction_id             (transaction_id)
  - idx_ops_kot_child_batch_no                   (batch_no)

Notes:
  - High-frequency POS child; kot_master_id always indexed; company_id + branch_id duplicated from parent by design.
  - android_printed BOOLEAN (not legacy text); money -> NUMERIC(18,2); datetime audit -> timestamp; snake_case.
  - Use with ops.kot_master for full KOT flow; FKs to product/group when masters are ready.

  - status                VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Operational LPO status
  - sync_status           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Upload/sync (legacy UploadStatusM)

  - created_by            VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by
  - created_at            TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at
  - modified_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by
  - modified_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at

  branch_id:  Yes (required)

  - idx_ops_lpo_master_branch_id                (branch_id)

  - idx_ops_lpo_master_salesman_id              (salesman_id)
  - idx_ops_lpo_master_order_no                 (order_no)
  - idx_ops_lpo_master_grn_no                   (grn_no)
  - idx_ops_lpo_master_lpo_date                 (lpo_date)
  - idx_ops_lpo_master_status                   (status)
  - idx_ops_lpo_master_sync_status              (sync_status)
  - idx_ops_lpo_master_record_status            (record_status)

Notes:

  - credit_card_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Card portion (legacy CreditCardAmount)

# ops.product_movement_register
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Inventory stock movement ledger: qty, running balance, opening stock,

              branch-level stock activity (generic document linkage).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.ProductMovementRegister (identity ID only; no separate movement key).

Primary key: id

Technical / business key:

    or sequence-assigned; set explicitly when backfilling migrated rows)

Columns:
  - id                   BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  PK (legacy ID)
  - movement_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Register line id (new; identity-style per company scope)

  - movement_date        TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  When movement occurred (legacy TransferDate)
  - movement_type        VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. SALE, PURCHASE, TRANSFER, ADJUSTMENT (legacy TransferType)
  - operand              VARCHAR(10)     NOT NULL  Direction e.g. +, -, IN, OUT (legacy Operand)
  - document_no          VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Source document no. (legacy DocumentNo)
  - master_id            BIGINT          NULL      Source header id (legacy MasterID)
  - child_id             BIGINT          NULL      Source line id (legacy ChildID)
  - qty                  NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Movement qty (legacy Qty)
  - stock_balance        NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Balance after line (legacy StockBalance)
  - last_purchase_cost   NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Last purchase cost snapshot (legacy LastPurchCost)
  - average_cost         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Average cost snapshot (legacy AverageCost)
  - pack_qty             NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Pack qty (legacy PackQty)
  - created_at           TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by           VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)

Foreign keys:
  - Optional when enforced: product_id -> core.product_master; branch_id -> branch master.

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_ops_product_movement_register_company_movement_id  (company_id, movement_id)

Constraints (check) (suggested):
  - chk_ops_product_movement_register_operand  Operand in allowed set (+, -, IN, OUT, etc.) per business rules

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_product_movement_register_company_product_id          (company_id, product_id)
  - idx_ops_product_movement_register_company_branch_product        (company_id, branch_id, product_id)

  - idx_ops_product_movement_register_company_movement_type         (company_id, movement_type)

  - idx_ops_product_movement_register_company_master_child          (company_id, master_id, child_id)

Notes:
  - Stock ledger in ops; TransferDate -> movement_date; TransferType -> movement_type; generic master_id/child_id for many sources;
    movement_id added where legacy had no business key; constrain operand to expected direction values in DDL.

    M-suffix tax/subtotal -> snake_case without M; restaurant/retail/delivery/online/garage fields kept for compatibility;
    modularize vertical extensions later if needed; blind MSSQL defaults not carried.

# ops.stock_adjustment
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Branch-level stock correction transactions: system vs physical qty, adjustment
              and pack qty, last purchase cost ref, reason, post/sync/counter-close/record
              status, audit — operational inventory (ops).

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.StockAdjustment (PK StockAdjustmentID + identity ID).

  - (company_id, branch_id, stock_adjustment_no) — document no. unique per branch

  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - stock_adjustment_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business adjustment id (legacy StockAdjustmentID)
  - stock_adjustment_no     NUMERIC(18,0)   NOT NULL  Adjustment doc no. (legacy stock adjustment no.)
  - adjustment_date         TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Transaction date/time (legacy AdjustmentDate)
  - remarks                 VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remarks)
  - product_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Product (legacy ProductID)
  - unique_product_id       BIGINT          NULL      Unique inventory ref (legacy UniqueProductId / similar)
  - present_stock           NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  System/on-hand (legacy PresentStock)
  - adjustment_qty          NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Correction qty (legacy AdjustmentQty)
  - pack_qty                NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Pack qty (legacy PackQty)
  - last_purchase_cost      NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Cost ref (legacy LastPurchaseCost)
  - physical_qty            NUMERIC(18,4)   NOT NULL  Physical count (legacy PhysicalQty)
  - reason                  VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Reason (legacy Reason)
  - post_status             VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  DRAFT / POSTED / CANCELLED (legacy PostStatus)
  - branch_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch/store (legacy StationID)
  - created_by              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - modified_by             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NULL      Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - counter_close_status    VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  OPEN / CLOSED (legacy CounterCloseStatus)
  - sync_status             VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL  PENDING / SYNCED / FAILED (legacy UploadStatus)
  - record_status           VARCHAR(15)     NOT NULL  ACTIVE / INACTIVE / DELETED (legacy RStatus; standardized)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - (company_id, product_id) -> core.product_master when enforced; branch_id -> branch master.

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_ops_stock_adjustment_company_stock_adjustment_id    (company_id, stock_adjustment_id)
  - uq_ops_stock_adjustment_company_branch_adjustment_no   (company_id, branch_id, stock_adjustment_no)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - present_stock, pack_qty, physical_qty, last_purchase_cost >= 0
  - record_status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','DELETED'); post_status IN ('DRAFT','POSTED','CANCELLED');
    sync_status IN ('PENDING','SYNCED','FAILED'); counter_close_status IN ('OPEN','CLOSED')
  - (adjustment_qty unrestricted — can be negative for reductions)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_branch                 (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_product_id             (company_id, product_id)
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_branch_product         (company_id, branch_id, product_id)
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_adjustment_date        (company_id, adjustment_date)
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_post_status            (company_id, post_status)
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_record_status          (company_id, record_status)
  - idx_ops_stock_adjustment_company_sync_status            (company_id, sync_status)

Notes:
  - Ops inventory correction; StationID -> branch_id; identity -> id; enforce CHECK/ENUM lists only if you want DB-level guards
    (defaults in sample DDL can be dropped if you prefer application-only lifecycle); align legacy column names during ETL;
    blind MSSQL defaults not carried unless you explicitly adopt the PostgreSQL defaults above.

  - idx_ops_transfer_detail_company_branch              (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_ops_transfer_detail_company_branch_transfer     (company_id, branch_id, transfer_id)
  - idx_ops_transfer_detail_company_branch_product      (company_id, branch_id, product_id)
  - idx_ops_transfer_detail_company_branch_post_status  (company_id, branch_id, post_status)

Notes:
  - TransferChild -> ops.transfer_detail; UploadStatus -> sync_status; RStatusC -> record_status; money -> NUMERIC(18,2);
    FK aligns detail branch_id with header when posting; removed blind defaults; snake_case.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ops.van_sale_settlement_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Van/mobile sales settlement header: document no./date, van, route,
              salesman, cash/credit/return/damage/discount/expense/short-excess,
              collected, receipt, advance, stock, net sale, post/sync/download —
              ops settlement workflow.

Legacy (MSSQL): dbo.VansaleSettlement (PK SettlementID + identity ID).

Primary key: id

Business keys (unique per company and branch):
  - settlement_id (legacy SettlementID)
  - (company_id, branch_id, settlement_no)

Columns:
  - id                      BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK (legacy ID)
  - company_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company (new)
  - branch_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch (legacy StationId)
  - settlement_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business settlement id (legacy SettlementID)
  - settlement_no           VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  Doc no. (legacy SettlementNo)
  - settlement_date         TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Settlement date (legacy SettlementDate)
  - van_no                  VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Van (legacy VanNo)
  - route_name              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Route (legacy Route)
  - salesman_id             BIGINT          NULL      Salesman (legacy SalesmanID)
  - cash_sale_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Cash sales (legacy CashSale)
  - credit_sale_amount      NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Credit sales (legacy CreditSale)
  - sales_return_amount     NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Returns (legacy SalesReturn)
  - damage_amount           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Damage (legacy Damage)
  - discount_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Discount (legacy Discount)
  - expense_amount          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Expense (legacy Expence; spelling fixed)
  - short_excess_amount     NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Short / excess (legacy ShortExcess; may be signed)
  - collected_amount        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Collected (legacy CollectedAmt)
  - receipt_amount          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Receipt (legacy Receipt)
  - advance_amount          NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Advance (legacy Advance)
  - stock_amount            NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Stock (legacy StockAmount)
  - net_sale_amount         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Net sale (legacy NetSale)
  - post_status             VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Posting (legacy PostStatus)
  - remarks                 VARCHAR(150)    NULL      Notes (legacy Remark)
  - sync_status             VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Sync (legacy UploadStatus)
  - download_status         VARCHAR(30)     NULL      Download (legacy DownloadStatus)
  - created_at              TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Created at (legacy CrOn)
  - created_by              VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by (legacy CrBy)
  - modified_at             TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Modified at (legacy ModOn)
  - modified_by             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Modified by (legacy ModBy)

Foreign keys (suggested):
  - branch_id -> branch master; salesman_id -> core.staff_master; van_no -> fleet/van master when modeled.

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_settlement_id  (company_id, branch_id, settlement_id)
  - uq_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_settlement_no  (company_id, branch_id, settlement_no)

Constraints (check) — optional in DDL:
  - cash_sale_amount, credit_sale_amount, sales_return_amount, damage_amount, discount_amount, expense_amount,
    collected_amount, receipt_amount, advance_amount, stock_amount, net_sale_amount >= 0
  - (short_excess_amount unrestricted — can be negative for shortage or positive for excess)
  - post_status IN ('DRAFT','POSTED','CANCELLED','APPROVED','PENDING')

Scope:
  company_id: Yes (required)
  branch_id:  Yes (required)

Indexes:
  - idx_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch              (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_date         (company_id, branch_id, settlement_date)
  - idx_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_salesman     (company_id, branch_id, salesman_id)
  - idx_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_van          (company_id, branch_id, van_no)
  - idx_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_post_status  (company_id, branch_id, post_status)
  - idx_ops_van_sale_settlement_master_company_branch_sync_status  (company_id, branch_id, sync_status)

Notes:
  - VansaleSettlement -> ops.van_sale_settlement_master; StationId -> branch_id; Route -> route_name; Expence -> expense_amount;
    Remark -> remarks; UploadStatus -> sync_status; money -> NUMERIC(18,2); nullable legacy amounts normalized to 0 in ETL;
    short_excess_amount not forced non-negative; van/staff/posting links when masters exist; snake_case.

# core.plan_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Moifone / SaaS pricing catalog: plan codes, display copy, prices (display
              strings + optional future billing fields), feature list JSON, default
              trial length, and optional validity window (effective_from / effective_to).
              Queried by public GET /api/plans (no auth). Registration validates plan_code
              against active rows.

Primary key: plan_code

Columns:
  - plan_code              VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  PK (e.g. basic, standard, pro, custom)
  - display_name           VARCHAR(100)    NOT NULL  Marketing name
  - audience_label         VARCHAR(100)    NULL      e.g. Best for startups
  - description            VARCHAR(500)    NULL      Short blurb
  - price_monthly_display  VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. $49 or Custom
  - price_yearly_display   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      e.g. $470/yr (display only)
  - period_label           VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL  e.g. month, talk to us
  - features               JSONB           NOT NULL  Bullet list for UI
  - card_note              VARCHAR(500)    NULL
  - cta_label              VARCHAR(80)     NULL      Button text
  - is_popular             BOOLEAN         NOT NULL
  - sort_order             SMALLINT        NOT NULL  Display order
  - trial_days             INTEGER         NOT NULL  Default trial length for new signups
  - effective_from         DATE            NULL      Catalog valid from (NULL = no lower bound)
  - effective_to           DATE            NULL      Catalog valid to (NULL = open-ended)
  - is_active              BOOLEAN         NOT NULL
  - created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ     NOT NULL
  - updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ     NOT NULL

Foreign keys:
  - None (referenced by core.company_onboarding.plan_code)

Scope:
  Global catalog (no company_id).

Indexes:
  - idx_plan_master_active_sort (is_active, sort_order)

Notes:
  - DDL + seed: database/migrations/002_plan_master.sql

  - is_active        BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Active flag

  - created_by       VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Created by

  - idx_attachment_master_company_branch_active (company_id, branch_id, is_active)

  - Raw binary file table is NOT migrated
  - Use file_url-based storage only

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, log_id)

Columns:
  - id           BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id   BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id    BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - log_id       BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business log id
  - employee_id  BIGINT          NOT NULL  Employee reference
  - punch_time   TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Punch timestamp
  - punch_type   VARCHAR(5)      NOT NULL  IN / OUT etc.
  - device_id    BIGINT          NULL      Device reference
  - sync_status  BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Synced flag
  - upload_time  TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Upload time

Foreign keys:
  - employee_id -> hr.employee_master(employee_id) [optional safe FK later]

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_attendance_log_id  (company_id, branch_id, log_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_attendance_log_company_branch      (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_attendance_log_company_branch_emp  (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)
  - idx_attendance_log_company_branch_time (company_id, branch_id, punch_time)

Notes:
  - Raw feed table for attendance processing
  - Used to build daily and pair summaries

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# hr.attendance_daily
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Daily attendance summary per employee including first in, last out,
              work hours, break hours, net hours, lateness, early leave, missing flag, and OT.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, daily_id)
  - (company_id, branch_id, employee_id, work_date)

Columns:
  - id               BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id       BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - daily_id         BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business daily summary id
  - employee_id      BIGINT          NOT NULL  Employee reference
  - work_date        DATE            NOT NULL  Work date
  - shift_id         BIGINT          NULL      Shift reference
  - first_in         TIMESTAMP       NULL      First IN
  - last_out         TIMESTAMP       NULL      Last OUT
  - total_work_hours NUMERIC(10,2)   NULL      Total worked hours
  - break_hours      NUMERIC(10,2)   NULL      Break hours
  - net_hours        NUMERIC(10,2)   NULL      Net hours
  - late_minutes     INTEGER         NULL      Late minutes
  - early_minutes    INTEGER         NULL      Early leave minutes
  - is_missing       BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  Missing punch flag
  - ot_hours         NUMERIC(10,2)   NULL      OT hours
  - attendance_status VARCHAR(20)    NOT NULL  Daily status

Foreign keys:
  - employee_id -> hr.employee_master(employee_id) [optional safe FK later]
  - shift_id -> hr.shift_master(shift_id) [optional safe FK later]

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_attendance_daily_id        (company_id, branch_id, daily_id)
  - uq_attendance_daily_emp_date  (company_id, branch_id, employee_id, work_date)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_attendance_daily_company_branch        (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_attendance_daily_company_branch_emp    (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)
  - idx_attendance_daily_company_branch_date   (company_id, branch_id, work_date)
  - idx_attendance_daily_company_branch_status (company_id, branch_id, attendance_status)

Notes:
  - Daily attendance summary table
  - Used for attendance review and payroll inputs

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# hr.attendance_pair
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     In/out attendance pair segments under a daily attendance row, useful
              for shift breaks, multiple in/out cycles, and detailed time analysis.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, pair_id)

Columns:
  - id                   BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - pair_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business pair id
  - daily_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Daily summary reference
  - pair_index           INTEGER         NOT NULL  Sequence number
  - in_time              TIMESTAMP       NULL      IN time
  - out_time             TIMESTAMP       NULL      OUT time
  - work_duration_hours  NUMERIC(10,2)   NULL      Work duration
  - break_duration_hours NUMERIC(10,2)   NULL      Break duration

Foreign keys:
  - daily_id -> hr.attendance_daily(daily_id) [optional safe FK later]

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_attendance_pair_id  (company_id, branch_id, pair_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_attendance_pair_company_branch       (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_attendance_pair_company_branch_daily (company_id, branch_id, daily_id)

Notes:
  - Detail-level attendance segments under daily summary

# hr.employee_pay_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Employee payroll setup/master including salary type, salary, OT rates,
              allowances, deductions, and bank/payment information.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, pay_employee_id)
  - (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)

Columns:
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - pay_employee_id        BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business payroll setup id
  - employee_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Employee reference
  - salary_type            VARCHAR(10)     NOT NULL  Salary type
  - basic_salary           NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Basic salary
  - ot_per_hour            NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  OT/hour
  - holiday_ot_per_hour    NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Holiday OT/hour
  - weekly_off_ot_per_hour NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Weekly off OT/hour
  - hra                    NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  HRA
  - transport_allowance    NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Transport allowance
  - food_allowance         NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Food allowance
  - other_allowance        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Other allowance
  - other_deduction        NUMERIC(18,2)   NOT NULL  Other deduction
  - is_ot_applicable       BOOLEAN         NOT NULL  OT applicable flag
  - pay_mode               VARCHAR(20)     NULL      Pay mode
  - bank_name              VARCHAR(100)    NULL      Bank name
  - account_no             VARCHAR(50)     NULL      Account number
  - iban                   VARCHAR(50)     NULL      IBAN

Foreign keys:
  - employee_id -> hr.employee_master(employee_id) [optional safe FK later]

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_employee_pay_master_id   (company_id, branch_id, pay_employee_id)
  - uq_employee_pay_master_emp  (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_employee_pay_master_company_branch     (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_employee_pay_master_company_branch_emp (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)

Notes:
  - One active payroll setup per employee is typical
  - Used by payroll processing

# hr.payroll_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Payroll processing header for a branch and pay period (month/year),
              including process date, status, and remarks.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, payroll_id)
  - (company_id, branch_id, month_no, year_no)

Columns:
  - id             BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id      BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - payroll_id     BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business payroll id
  - month_no       INTEGER         NOT NULL  Month number
  - year_no        INTEGER         NOT NULL  Year
  - process_date   TIMESTAMP       NOT NULL  Process date
  - payroll_status VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Payroll status
  - remarks        VARCHAR(200)    NULL      Notes

Foreign keys:
  - none

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_payroll_master_id      (company_id, branch_id, payroll_id)
  - uq_payroll_master_period  (company_id, branch_id, month_no, year_no)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_payroll_master_company_branch        (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_payroll_master_company_branch_status (company_id, branch_id, payroll_status)

Notes:
  - Header/master payroll process table
  - Parent of hr.payroll_detail

# hr.payroll_detail
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Employee-wise payroll calculation details under payroll process header:
              days, hours, allowances, deductions, gross pay, and net pay.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, payroll_detail_id)

Columns:
  - id                  BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - payroll_detail_id   BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business payroll detail id
  - payroll_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Payroll header reference
  - employee_id         BIGINT          NOT NULL  Employee reference
  - present_days        NUMERIC(10,2)   NOT NULL  Present days
  - absent_days         NUMERIC(10,2)   NOT NULL  Absent days
  - leave_days          NUMERIC(10,2)   NOT NULL  Leave days
  - ot_hours            NUMERIC(10,2)   NOT NULL  OT hours
  - holiday_ot_hours    NUMERIC(10,2)   NOT NULL  Holiday OT hours
  - weekly_off_hours    NUMERIC(10,2)   NOT NULL  Weekly off hours
  - basic_pay           NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Basic pay
  - ot_pay              NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  OT pay
  - holiday_ot_pay      NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Holiday OT pay
  - weekly_off_ot_pay   NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Weekly off OT pay
  - allowance_total     NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Allowance total
  - loan_deduction      NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Loan deduction
  - other_deduction     NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Other deduction
  - gross_pay           NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Gross pay
  - net_pay             NUMERIC(18,3)   NOT NULL  Net pay
  - payroll_status      VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL  Payroll line status

Foreign keys:
  - payroll_id -> hr.payroll_master(payroll_id) [optional safe FK later]
  - employee_id -> hr.employee_master(employee_id) [optional safe FK later]

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_payroll_detail_id  (company_id, branch_id, payroll_detail_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_payroll_detail_company_branch         (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_payroll_detail_company_branch_payroll (company_id, branch_id, payroll_id)
  - idx_payroll_detail_company_branch_emp     (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)

Notes:
  - Detail table under hr.payroll_master
  - Used for payroll review and payslip output

# hr.shift_master
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Shift setup/master including shift name, type, timings, minimum hours,
              grace settings, break settings, and OT start rule.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, shift_id)
  - (company_id, branch_id, shift_name)

Columns:
  - id                     BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id             BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id              BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - shift_id               BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business shift id
  - shift_name             VARCHAR(100)    NOT NULL  Shift name
  - shift_type             VARCHAR(10)     NOT NULL  Shift type
  - start_time             TIME            NULL      Shift start
  - end_time               TIME            NULL      Shift end
  - min_work_hours         NUMERIC(5,2)    NOT NULL  Minimum work hours
  - late_grace_minutes     INTEGER         NOT NULL  Late grace
  - early_grace_minutes    INTEGER         NOT NULL  Early grace
  - auto_break_minutes     INTEGER         NOT NULL  Auto break
  - ot_start_after_minutes INTEGER         NOT NULL  OT starts after

Foreign keys:
  - none

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_shift_master_id    (company_id, branch_id, shift_id)
  - uq_shift_master_name  (company_id, branch_id, shift_name)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_shift_master_company_branch (company_id, branch_id)

Notes:
  - Used by employee master, attendance daily, and shift assignment

# hr.shift_assignment
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Purpose:     Employee-to-shift assignment history with effective date ranges.

Primary key: id

Business keys:
  - (company_id, branch_id, shift_assignment_id)

Columns:
  - id                  BIGSERIAL       NOT NULL  Internal PK
  - company_id          BIGINT          NOT NULL  Owning company
  - branch_id           BIGINT          NOT NULL  Branch / location
  - shift_assignment_id BIGINT          NOT NULL  Business shift assignment id
  - employee_id         BIGINT          NOT NULL  Employee reference
  - shift_id            BIGINT          NOT NULL  Shift reference
  - from_date           DATE            NOT NULL  Effective from
  - to_date             DATE            NULL      Effective to

Foreign keys:
  - employee_id -> hr.employee_master(employee_id) [optional safe FK later]
  - shift_id -> hr.shift_master(shift_id) [optional safe FK later]

Constraints (unique):
  - uq_shift_assignment_id  (company_id, branch_id, shift_assignment_id)

Scope:
  company_id: Yes
  branch_id:  Yes

Indexes:
  - idx_shift_assignment_company_branch       (company_id, branch_id)
  - idx_shift_assignment_company_branch_emp   (company_id, branch_id, employee_id)
  - idx_shift_assignment_company_branch_shift (company_id, branch_id, shift_id)

Notes:
  - Historical / effective-dated shift assignment table
